2. **`خ و ف` — `B001` (anticipation of harm), `ش ي ء` — `B001`/`B002` (knowable thing / will toward a thing), `و س ع` — `B001`/`B002` (spaciousness / capacity to contain), and `ع ل م` — `B001` (clear knowledge).** **Cue:** partner-fear is negated except under the Lord's will, then knowledge is said to contain every thing; `6:81` reverses the fear-question onto the community. **Role:** comprehensive knowledge encloses every possible harmful event inside divine will, leaving partners no independent threat-space. **Abductive move:** I infer that fear is rational only where causal agency remains outside a trusted containing order; will, all things, and spacious knowledge are supplied, while threat-space exclusion is mine. **Reading change:** Abraham's fearlessness is grounded in causal containment, not bravado.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:81–82` reversed the fear question and answered it, showing that comprehensive causal containment yields security only when trust is not mixed with misplaced allegiance.

## 6:81 — وَكَيْفَ أَخَافُ مَآ أَشْرَكْتُمْ وَلَا تَخَافُونَ أَنَّكُمْ أَشْرَكْتُم بِٱللَّهِ مَا لَمْ يُنَزِّلْ بِهِۦ عَلَيْكُمْ سُلْطَٰنًۭا ۚ فَأَىُّ ٱلْفَرِيقَيْنِ أَحَقُّ بِٱلْأَمْنِ ۖ إِن كُنتُمْ تَعْلَمُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`س ل ط` — `B002`/`B003` (overpowering proof / authority), `ن ز ل` — `B002` (sending down), and `ش ر ك` — `B001` (partnership).** **Cue:** the community fears its partners though no authorizing proof has descended for them; `6:83` will call Abraham's reasoning a divinely given proof. **Role:** valid authority requires an evidentiary chain from above, whereas their partnership has social force without warrant. **Abductive move:** I infer that unauthorized fear is produced by communal enforcement filling the gap left by absent proof; fear, partnership, absent descent, and coming divine argument are supplied, while social-force substitution is mine. **Reading change:** the verse audits not whether idols inspire fear but whether that fear carries traceable authority.

2. **`ف ر ق` — `B001`/`B005`/`B009` (distinguishing / two parties / heart split by fear), `ح ق ق` — `B002` (deserved claim), and `ء م ن` — `B001` (settled security).** **Cue:** two parties are distinguished by opposite fear structures; `6:82` immediately identifies the secure party by unmixed trust. **Role:** fear splits the heart and the parties, while security belongs by right to the relation backed by proof. **Abductive move:** I infer that security is an evidentiary entitlement, not a subjective feeling distributed equally; party contrast, right, and next-ayah answer are supplied, while entitlement is mine. **Reading change:** `أَحَقُّ بِٱلْأَمْنِ` makes safety the outcome of a valid authority relation.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:82–83` supplied the answer and its warrant: security belongs to unmixed trust because the opposing argument lacks descended authority while Abraham's proof is explicitly granted.

## 6:82 — ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ وَلَمْ يَلْبِسُوٓا۟ إِيمَٰنَهُم بِظُلْمٍ أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ لَهُمُ ٱلْأَمْنُ وَهُم مُّهْتَدُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ء م ن` — `B001`/`B002` (settled security and trust / trusting assent), `ل ب س` — `B001`/`B003` (covering garment / confusing mixture), and `ظ ل م` — `B004`/`B008` (misplacing / withholding).** **Cue:** the root of trust/security occurs three times around a prohibition on clothing or mixing it with injustice; `6:81` asked which party owns security, and `6:83` identifies the proof supporting Abraham. **Role:** trust is capable of yielding security, but misplaced allegiance can wrap and confuse it until that effect is withheld. **Abductive move:** I infer that injustice does not merely sit beside belief but interrupts the trust-to-security conversion; repeated root, mixing, and security are supplied, while interruption is mine. **Reading change:** the ayah describes security as the functional output of unadulterated allegiance.

2. **`ه د ي` — `B001`/`B002` (gentle guidance / intended course).** **Cue:** guidance closes the answer after Abraham's bodily reorientation and the two-party dispute; the next ayah depicts proof as rising through degrees. **Role:** guidance is the maintained course on which unmixed trust can settle rather than oscillate among feared powers. **Abductive move:** I infer that security and guidance mutually stabilize one another: proof clears fear, and settled trust preserves direction; both outcomes are supplied, while reciprocal stabilization is mine. **Reading change:** being guided is not an extra reward after security but its directional form.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:83` made the trust-to-security conversion epistemically visible: granted proof rises by degrees and supplies the intended course on which trust can settle.

## 6:83 — وَتِلْكَ حُجَّتُنَآ ءَاتَيْنَٰهَآ إِبْرَٰهِيمَ عَلَىٰ قَوْمِهِۦ ۚ نَرْفَعُ دَرَجَٰتٍۢ مَّن نَّشَآءُ ۗ إِنَّ رَبَّكَ حَكِيمٌ عَلِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **`ح ج ج` — `B002`/`B003` (clear intended road / prevailing proof), `ء ت ي` — `B002` (granting and bringing), and `ق و م` — `B014` (facing off).** **Cue:** Abraham's multi-stage observation and debate are retrospectively named a granted argument over his disputing people; `6:84–87` then extend guidance through a lineage. **Role:** proof is a navigable route through the dispute, but its availability is received rather than self-generated possession. **Abductive move:** I infer that the earlier experimental sequence is itself the form in which the grant arrived; showing, reasoning, argument, and explicit gift are supplied, while sequence-as-grant is mine. **Reading change:** the argument's force lies in being led through a road of observations, not only in a final proposition.

2. **`ر ف ع` — `B001`/`B002` (raising upward / rank), `د ر ج` — `B001`/`B003` (walking a path / step by step), `ح ك م` — `B003` (wise rightly guided knowledge), and `ع ل م` — `B001` (clear knowledge).** **Cue:** raising degrees follows an argument that unfolded by star, moon, sun, orientation, and debate; the next ayat list guided generations. **Role:** degrees are steps on an epistemic-ethical path, while wisdom orders their sequence and knowledge identifies their proper elevation. **Abductive move:** I infer that rank is produced through guided stages while elevation remains divinely assigned; stepwise reasoning and raising are supplied, while stage-to-rank relation is mine. **Reading change:** “raising degrees” becomes structured advancement through granted insight, not arbitrary status inflation.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:84–87` extended raised degrees into a guided lineage, confirming that advancement unfolds through granted stages that can spread across generations.

## 6:84 — وَوَهَبْنَا لَهُۥٓ إِسْحَٰقَ وَيَعْقُوبَ ۚ كُلًّا هَدَيْنَا ۚ وَنُوحًا هَدَيْنَا مِن قَبْلُ ۖ وَمِن ذُرِّيَّتِهِۦ دَاوُۥدَ وَسُلَيْمَٰنَ وَأَيُّوبَ وَيُوسُفَ وَمُوسَىٰ وَهَٰرُونَ ۚ وَكَذَٰلِكَ نَجْزِى ٱلْمُحْسِنِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`و ه ب` — `B001`/`B007` (gift without exchange / what lasts for someone), `ذ ر ر` — `B001`/`B004` (scattered motes / gentle spreading emergence), and `ه د ي` — `B001` (gentle guidance).** **Cue:** gifted sons open a list that spreads backward to Noah and outward through descendants; `6:87` explicitly gathers fathers, offspring, and brothers. **Role:** gift becomes durable by dispersing guidance through persons who emerge across generations. **Abductive move:** I infer that the lasting dimension of the gift is not biological continuity alone but recurrent guidance within dispersed lineage; gift, descendants, and repeated guidance are supplied, while guidance-as-durability is mine. **Reading change:** the genealogy reads as a spreading gift-network rather than a prestige list.

2. **`ج ز ي` — `B001`/`B002` (recompense matching an act / standing in and sufficing) and `ح س ن` — `B002` (skillful beneficent action).** **Cue:** the list closes by identifying this as how doers of good are recompensed, after beginning with unexchanged gift; `6:88` warns that partnership would nullify their works. **Role:** grace supplies the lineage and guidance, while recompense answers the good action that operates within that gift. **Abductive move:** I infer a noncompetitive relation between gift and response: unearned provision enables action whose quality still matters; both gift and recompense are supplied, while enabling relation is mine. **Reading change:** the ayah holds inheritance and accountability together without reducing one to the other.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:87–88` clarified the gift's durability: kin are gathered onto one course, yet partnership could still nullify their work, so continuity is guided rather than biologically guaranteed.

## 6:85 — وَزَكَرِيَّا وَيَحْيَىٰ وَعِيسَىٰ وَإِلْيَاسَ ۖ كُلٌّۭ مِّنَ ٱلصَّٰلِحِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ك ل ل` — `B003`/`B009` (totality / gathered groups) and `ص ل ح` — `B001`/`B003` (soundness against corruption / fitness for a function).** **Cue:** four names form one unit inside the larger guided genealogy, and each is included without gradation; `6:86` then adds another group and shared preference. **Role:** totality preserves individual inclusion, while sound fitness supplies the common functional property joining them. **Abductive move:** I infer that the list constructs a distributed body whose unity lies in sound function rather than sameness of circumstance; names, totality, and righteousness are supplied, while distributed-body model is mine. **Reading change:** `كُلٌّۭ مِّنَ ٱلصَّٰلِحِينَ` is a compact systems claim: every named member is fit within the guidance network.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:86–87` confirmed the distributed-body reading by adding another complete group and then gathering diverse kin into one straight functional course.

## 6:86 — وَإِسْمَٰعِيلَ وَٱلْيَسَعَ وَيُونُسَ وَلُوطًۭا ۚ وَكُلًّۭا فَضَّلْنَا عَلَى ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ك ل ل` — `B003` (totality), `ف ض ل` — `B001`/`B002`/`B003` (excess or remainder / rank / bounty), and `ع ل م` — `B002` (distinguishing mark).** **Cue:** another set of individual names is included in a collective preference; `6:83` spoke of raised degrees, while `6:87` gathers wider kin and `6:88` conditions work on nonpartnership. **Role:** bounty supplies an excess that distinguishes and raises without making rank an inherited possession. **Abductive move:** I infer that preference is surplus gift expressed through marked function, not competitive scarcity among worlds; totality, preference, and nearby gift language are supplied, while non-scarcity is mine. **Reading change:** `فَضَّلْنَا` emphasizes bestowed excess that equips the whole listed group.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:87–88` qualified preference as equipping bounty rather than owned rank, because selection gathers across kin and guidance remains contingent on divine willing.

## 6:87 — وَمِنْ ءَابَآئِهِمْ وَذُرِّيَّٰتِهِمْ وَإِخْوَٰنِهِمْ ۖ وَٱجْتَبَيْنَٰهُمْ وَهَدَيْنَٰهُمْ إِلَىٰ صِرَٰطٍۢ مُّسْتَقِيمٍۢ

### Activated readings

1. **`ء ب و` — `B001` (parental nurture), `ذ ر ر` — `B004` (gentle spreading emergence), `ء خ و` — `B001`/`B002` (kinship / tethering bond), and `ج ب ي` — `B001`/`B004` (gathering / selecting and drawing near).** **Cue:** ancestors, descendants, and brothers span every direction of kinship, then selection gathers “them” into one object; `6:88` says guidance still depends on divine will. **Role:** dispersive lineage is recollected from its temporal and lateral branches, while selection draws particular persons near. **Abductive move:** I infer that chosen continuity is assembled across kin structures rather than automatically transmitted by them; kin categories, gathering-selection, and guidance are supplied, while nonautomatic assembly is mine. **Reading change:** genealogy becomes raw dispersed material that selection gathers, not the cause that guarantees selection.

2. **`ه د ي` — `B001`/`B002` (gentle guidance / intended course), `ص ر ط` — `B001`/`B002` (straight road / disappearing in passage), and `ق و م` — `B008` (straightness).** **Cue:** gathered kin are guided “to” one straight road after multiple lists, and the next ayah identifies this route as God's guidance. **Role:** the road absorbs diverse biographies into a common passage whose defining action is continual straightening. **Abductive move:** I infer that unity lies in direction rather than ancestry or era; diverse kin and singular road are supplied, while directional unity is mine. **Reading change:** the lineage list resolves into a traversable common course instead of a static family tree.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:88` explicitly prevented lineage from becoming entitlement: the common road is God's guidance and remains effective only under undivided allegiance.

## 6:88 — ذَٰلِكَ هُدَى ٱللَّهِ يَهْدِى بِهِۦ مَن يَشَآءُ مِنْ عِبَادِهِۦ ۚ وَلَوْ أَشْرَكُوا۟ لَحَبِطَ عَنْهُم مَّا كَانُوا۟ يَعْمَلُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ه د ي` — `B001`/`B002` (gentle guidance / intended course), `ش ي ء` — the `B001` will variant, and `ع ب د` — `B003` (submissive obedience).** **Cue:** repeated lineage guidance is attributed directly to divine willing rather than ancestry; `6:89` shows revelation entrusted to another people if current recipients cover it. **Role:** willing selects servants into a course, while servanthood keeps the course relational rather than hereditary. **Abductive move:** I infer that lineage displays transmission opportunities but never owns the guidance passing through it; genealogies, divine guidance, and coming replacement are supplied, while opportunity-versus-ownership is mine. **Reading change:** `ذَٰلِكَ هُدَى ٱللَّهِ` retrospectively prevents the prophet list from becoming a bloodline entitlement.

2. **`ش ر ك` — `B001` (partnership), `ح ب ط` — `B001`/`B004`/`B005` (harmful belly-swelling / relapse effects / disappearance of well water), and `ع م ل` — `B001` (intentional work).** **Cue:** even the honored guided figures are placed under a counterfactual condition: partnership would nullify their work; `6:89` similarly makes scripture custody replaceable. **Role:** partnership corrupts the intake relation so work can swell outward while losing nourishing value, like harmful grazing or a well whose water vanishes. **Abductive move:** I infer that correct action derives efficacy from undivided orientation, so mixed source-allegiance drains it; partnership, work, and nullification are supplied, while orientation-to-efficacy is mine. **Reading change:** no accumulated rank immunizes work from systemic corruption at its source.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:89` confirmed both claims: guidance custody can move to another people, and no inherited or accumulated status protects work whose source-relation is corrupted.

## 6:89 — أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ ٱلَّذِينَ ءَاتَيْنَٰهُمُ ٱلْكِتَٰبَ وَٱلْحُكْمَ وَٱلنُّبُوَّةَ ۚ فَإِن يَكْفُرْ بِهَا هَٰٓؤُلَآءِ فَقَدْ وَكَّلْنَا بِهَا قَوْمًۭا لَّيْسُوا۟ بِهَا بِكَٰفِرِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ك ت ب` — `B001`/`B002` (joining / written book), `ح ك م` — `B003`/`B004` (wise knowledge / decisive firmness), and `ن ب ء` — `B002`/`B003` (reported news / divine informing and prophethood).** **Cue:** the three gifts summarize the guided lineage, then their custody is made independent of the present audience; `6:90` turns that lineage into an exemplar and reminder. **Role:** the book joins transmitted material, judgment gives it rightly ordered application, and prophethood carries it as living report. **Abductive move:** I infer that these are interlocking custody functions rather than three prestige items; gift, guidance, transmission, and replacement are supplied, while functional interlock is mine. **Reading change:** the triad reads as a durable knowledge system capable of surviving any one audience.

2. **`ك ف ر` — `B001`/`B004` (covering / covering over a favor) and `و ك ل` — `B001`/`B006` (handing a matter over / one who undertakes and guards).** **Cue:** if “these” cover the gifts, another people are already entrusted who will not cover them; `6:91` shows selective exposure and concealment of scripture. **Role:** rejection is failed custodianship, while entrustment routes the same deposit to guardians who keep it operative. **Abductive move:** I infer a redundancy mechanism in revelation transmission: custody shifts when one node suppresses the material; covering, entrustment, and alternate people are supplied, while network redundancy is mine. **Reading change:** disbelief cannot extinguish the gift; it disqualifies one custodian and activates another.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:90–91` made the redundancy mechanism visible: the guided lineage becomes an exemplar, while selective exposure and concealment identify exactly how one custodian can fail the joined deposit.

## 6:90 — أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ ٱلَّذِينَ هَدَى ٱللَّهُ ۖ فَبِهُدَىٰهُمُ ٱقْتَدِهْ ۗ قُل لَّآ أَسْـَٔلُكُمْ عَلَيْهِ أَجْرًا ۖ إِنْ هُوَ إِلَّا ذِكْرَىٰ لِلْعَٰلَمِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ق د و` — `B001`/`B003` (exemplar followed / steady course in conduct) and `ه د ي` — `B002` (intended direction).** **Cue:** many individually named figures are reduced to one followable guidance, not to imitation of personal detail; `6:92` presents the new book as confirming what is before it. **Role:** exemplar extracts the stable course shared across diverse biographies. **Abductive move:** I infer that the command is to follow the invariant directional pattern, because no single listed life can literally be copied in full; plurality, shared guidance, and singular imperative are supplied, while invariant extraction is mine. **Reading change:** `ٱقْتَدِهْ` is disciplined pattern-following across difference, not personality replication.

2. **`س ء ل` — `B001` (asking), `ء ج ر` — `B001` (recompense for work), `ذ ك ر` — `B009` (reminder), and `ع ل م` — `B002` (distinguishing mark).** **Cue:** payment is negated before the message is universalized as reminder; `6:91` criticizes those who partition a book into selectively shown sheets. **Role:** absence of wage prevents access from becoming a transaction, while reminder lets the signs circulate across worlds. **Abductive move:** I infer that noncommercial delivery protects the message from ownership-based filtering; no wage, reminder, and coming selective concealment are supplied, while access-protection is mine. **Reading change:** the messenger's nonrequest is part of the reminder's universal transmission architecture.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:91–92` clarified the invariant being followed: selective sheets corrupt access, while the confirming book preserves one cross-generational directional pattern without becoming a commercial possession.

## 6:91 — وَمَا قَدَرُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ حَقَّ قَدْرِهِۦٓ إِذْ قَالُوا۟ مَآ أَنزَلَ ٱللَّهُ عَلَىٰ بَشَرٍۢ مِّن شَىْءٍۢ ۗ قُلْ مَنْ أَنزَلَ ٱلْكِتَٰبَ ٱلَّذِى جَآءَ بِهِۦ مُوسَىٰ نُورًۭا وَهُدًۭى لِّلنَّاسِ ۖ تَجْعَلُونَهُۥ قَرَاطِيسَ تُبْدُونَهَا وَتُخْفُونَ كَثِيرًۭا ۖ وَعُلِّمْتُم مَّا لَمْ تَعْلَمُوٓا۟ أَنتُمْ وَلَآ ءَابَآؤُكُمْ ۖ قُلِ ٱللَّهُ ۖ ثُمَّ ذَرْهُمْ فِى خَوْضِهِمْ يَلْعَبُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ق د ر` — `B001`/`B006` (measure reaching an extent / fitting proportion), `ح ق ق` — `B002` (due claim), `ن ز ل` — `B002` (sending down), and `ب ش ر` — `B001` (visible human skin or surface).** **Cue:** failure to measure God properly is expressed as denying any descent onto a human surface, but a concrete book and acquired knowledge answer the denial; `6:92` immediately presents another descended book. **Role:** mismeasurement imposes a false limit between divine source and embodied recipient. **Abductive move:** I infer that they mistake human visibility and finitude for incapacity to receive transmitted knowledge; descent, human recipient, book, and learning are supplied, while category-limit error is mine. **Reading change:** the theological failure is a bad measurement of communicative reach.

2. **`ق ر ط س` — `B001`/`B002` (writing sheet / target surface), `ب د و` — `B001` (visible emergence), `خ ف ي` — `B001` (hiddenness), `ك ث ر` — `B003` (abundance attached to claims), and `خ و ض` — `B001`/`B003` (wading / stirring a mixture).** **Cue:** one book is made into sheets, some displayed and much hidden; they are then left in wading play, while `6:92` emphasizes confirmation and preservation. **Role:** fragmentation creates surfaces that can be selectively targeted for exposure, and discursive stirring obscures the omitted whole. **Abductive move:** I infer that material partition enables epistemic curation: custodians can stage visibility sheet by sheet; sheets, showing/hiding, and play are supplied, while curation is mine. **Reading change:** concealment is an information-architecture practice, not only refusal to speak.

3. **`ن و ر` — `B005` (beacon or landmark), `ه د ي` — `B001`/`B002` (guidance / course), and `ع ل م` — `B001` (knowing clearly).** **Cue:** the book arrives as navigational light and teaches what neither audience nor ancestors knew; `6:97` will make stars navigational markers. **Role:** light marks a route through inherited ignorance rather than merely illuminating existing knowledge. **Abductive move:** I infer that selective concealment disables navigation by removing landmarks; beacon, guidance, learning, and hidden sheets are supplied, while landmark-removal is mine. **Reading change:** hiding scripture becomes route sabotage across generations.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:92` directly refuted the alleged limit on divine-human communication, and its blessed confirming descent showed that proper measure includes productive continuity across embodied recipients.

## 6:92 — وَهَٰذَا كِتَٰبٌ أَنزَلْنَٰهُ مُبَارَكٌۭ مُّصَدِّقُ ٱلَّذِى بَيْنَ يَدَيْهِ وَلِتُنذِرَ أُمَّ ٱلْقُرَىٰ وَمَنْ حَوْلَهَا ۚ وَٱلَّذِينَ يُؤْمِنُونَ بِٱلْءَاخِرَةِ يُؤْمِنُونَ بِهِۦ ۖ وَهُمْ عَلَىٰ صَلَاتِهِمْ يُحَافِظُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ب ر ك` — `B001`/`B004`/`B006` (settling / fixed growing good / holding fast), `ك ت ب` — `B001` (joining), and `ص د ق` — `B004` (confirmation and fulfillment).** **Cue:** this descended book settles into relation with what lies before it rather than replacing the prior record; `6:91` exposed fragmentation, and `6:93` exposes fabricated rivals. **Role:** blessing fixes the book in place while allowing good to grow through confirmatory joins. **Abductive move:** I infer that continuity supplies the stable base from which the new warning expands; descent, confirmation, and blessing are supplied, while stability-to-growth is mine. **Reading change:** `مُبَارَكٌۭ مُّصَدِّقُ` describes productive continuity, not passive endorsement.

2. **`ء م م` — `B002` (mother-source and gathering point), `ق ر ي` — `B001`/`B006` (gathered settlement / common course), and `ح و ل` — `B004` (space around a thing).** **Cue:** warning begins at the mother of settlements and extends around it; later `6:95–99` show life radiating from kernels, dawn, stars, one self, and water. **Role:** the city is a gathering center from which warning propagates concentrically to surrounding communities. **Abductive move:** I infer a center-to-periphery dissemination model; mother-city and surrounding space are supplied, while propagation is mine. **Reading change:** geography becomes transmission structure rather than a list of audiences.

3. **`ص ل و` — `B003` (prescribed worship) and `ح ف ظ` — `B001`/`B003` (preserving / steady observance).** **Cue:** belief in the later life and in the book produces continuous custody of prayer; the prior ayah criticized selective textual custody, and `6:94` strips away unpreserved affiliations. **Role:** ritual preservation is the embodied counterpart to preserving revelation whole. **Abductive move:** I infer that confidence in final return stabilizes repeated observance; afterlife belief, book belief, and prayer custody are supplied, while temporal stabilization is mine. **Reading change:** guarding prayer becomes a test of whether the book's future horizon has been operationally preserved.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:95–97` expanded the center-to-periphery mechanism: kernels, dawn, and stars each turn a fixed source or marker into radiating life, time, or direction.

## 6:93 — وَمَنْ أَظْلَمُ مِمَّنِ ٱفْتَرَىٰ عَلَى ٱللَّهِ كَذِبًا أَوْ قَالَ أُوحِىَ إِلَىَّ وَلَمْ يُوحَ إِلَيْهِ شَىْءٌۭ وَمَن قَالَ سَأُنزِلُ مِثْلَ مَآ أَنزَلَ ٱللَّهُ ۗ وَلَوْ تَرَىٰٓ إِذِ ٱلظَّٰلِمُونَ فِى غَمَرَٰتِ ٱلْمَوْتِ وَٱلْمَلَٰٓئِكَةُ بَاسِطُوٓا۟ أَيْدِيهِمْ أَخْرِجُوٓا۟ أَنفُسَكُمُ ۖ ٱلْيَوْمَ تُجْزَوْنَ عَذَابَ ٱلْهُونِ بِمَا كُنتُمْ تَقُولُونَ عَلَى ٱللَّهِ غَيْرَ ٱلْحَقِّ وَكُنتُمْ عَنْ ءَايَٰتِهِۦ تَسْتَكْبِرُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ف ر ي` — `B001`/`B003` (cutting and stitching / fabricating), `و ح ي` — `B001` (hidden imparting), `ن ز ل` — `B002` (sending down), and `م ث ل` — `B001`/`B008` (counterpart / representation).** **Cue:** false speakers claim either hidden reception or power to produce a matching descent after the prior ayat established an entrusted, confirming chain. **Role:** fabrication cuts visible pieces from revelation and restitches them into a counterfeit claim of source-access. **Abductive move:** I infer imitation from outputs without possession of the hidden transmission relation; claimed revelation, claimed likeness, and fabrication are supplied, while output-copying is mine. **Reading change:** the lie is precisely a forged provenance claim, not merely stylistic imitation.

2. **`غ م ر` — `B001`/`B003`/`B004` (overwhelming water / covering haze of falsehood / severity of death), `ب س ط` — `B004` (extending the hand), `خ ر ج` — `B001`/`B002` (passing out / causing to come out), and `ن ف س` — `B011` (living self).** **Cue:** those who claimed control over inward descent are submerged at death while angels extend hands and command them to output their own selves; `6:94` then shows them arriving alone. **Role:** the covering flood removes fabricated control, the extended hands demand transfer, and the self is the one output they cannot author. **Abductive move:** I infer a deliberate reversal between falsely bringing revelation down and being unable to bring the soul out; both speech claims and extraction command are supplied, while reversal is mine. **Reading change:** death tests claimed source-power at the boundary of the claimant's own interior.

3. **`ه و ن` — `B003` (humiliation through contempt) and `ك ب ر` — `B006` (self-magnification).** **Cue:** humiliating punishment is explicitly tied to speaking other than truth and magnifying oneself above signs; the next ayah removes possessions, partners, and social ties. **Role:** humiliation deflates a status built by forged revelation and superiority. **Abductive move:** I infer that the penalty mirrors the social elevation sought by the lie; self-magnification and abasement are supplied, while mirroring is mine. **Reading change:** `عَذَابَ ٱلْهُونِ` is the collapse of manufactured authority as well as pain.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:94` completed the death-scene reversals: the speaker who claimed to output revelation arrives with only the extracted self, stripped of the authority, property, and partners that magnified the claim.

## 6:94 — وَلَقَدْ جِئْتُمُونَا فُرَٰدَىٰ كَمَا خَلَقْنَٰكُمْ أَوَّلَ مَرَّةٍۢ وَتَرَكْتُم مَّا خَوَّلْنَٰكُمْ وَرَآءَ ظُهُورِكُمْ ۖ وَمَا نَرَىٰ مَعَكُمْ شُفَعَآءَكُمُ ٱلَّذِينَ زَعَمْتُمْ أَنَّهُمْ فِيكُمْ شُرَكَٰٓؤُا۟ ۚ لَقَد تَّقَطَّعَ بَيْنَكُمْ وَضَلَّ عَنكُم مَّا كُنتُمْ تَزْعُمُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ف ر د` — `B001`/`B002` (single apartness / separated bead), `خ ل ق` — `B001`/`B002` (proportioning / originating), and `م ر ر` — `B002` (an occurrence or repeated time).** **Cue:** arrival alone is measured against the first creation-event; `6:93` has extracted the self, while `6:95` splits solitary kernels to release life. **Role:** individuality is the durable unit that survives the stripping of acquired social stringing. **Abductive move:** I infer that “as first created” resets each person to the accountable unit beneath later affiliations; aloneness, first creation, and abandoned attachments are supplied, while reset-to-unit is mine. **Reading change:** resurrection is de-layering to the original created bead, not merely solitary attendance.

2. **`ت ر ك` — `B006` (what the dead leave), `خ و ل` — `B002`/`B003` (conferred possession / dependents and property), `و ر ي` — `B006` (the far side or behind), and `ظ ه ر` — `B002`/`B006` (back / support).** **Cue:** endowed possessions are left behind backs, immediately after death extraction; alleged advocates are also absent. **Role:** what once functioned as backing and support is spatially relegated beyond the returning self. **Abductive move:** I infer that death reverses possessions from carried support into inaccessible estate; conferral, leaving, behindness, and backs are supplied, while reversal-of-support is mine. **Reading change:** the scene makes property loss bodily: the support field has moved to the wrong side of the journey.

3. **`ش ف ع` — `B001`/`B002` (joining a like / advocate), `ق ط ع` — `B007` (severance of relation), `ب ي ن` — `B003` (tie between parties), `ض ل ل` — `B002` (disappearance), and `ز ع م` — `B003` (claimed surety).** **Cue:** partners and advocates are searched for, but the inter-party tie is cut and the claimed sureties disappear. **Role:** intercession requires a joining relation; severance removes the channel through which advocacy could operate. **Abductive move:** I infer that the failure is structural before it is rhetorical: there is no surviving linkage to carry the plea; absent advocates, cut tie, and vanished claims are supplied, while channel-failure is mine. **Reading change:** partnership collapses as a disconnected network, not just a set of missing figures.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:95` made the original “single bead” generative rather than merely isolated: solitary kernels are split so new life emerges, while accountable identity remains the unit carried through transition.

## 6:95 — ۞ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ فَالِقُ ٱلْحَبِّ وَٱلنَّوَىٰ ۖ يُخْرِجُ ٱلْحَىَّ مِنَ ٱلْمَيِّتِ وَمُخْرِجُ ٱلْمَيِّتِ مِنَ ٱلْحَىِّ ۚ ذَٰلِكُمُ ٱللَّهُ ۖ فَأَنَّىٰ تُؤْفَكُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ف ل ق` — `B001`/`B003` (cleaving between parts / bringing creation out by splitting), `ح ب ب` — `B001`/`B004` (seed-kernel / heart's inner kernel), `ن و ي` — `B002`/`B003` (inner purpose / fruit pit), and `خ ر ج` — `B001`/`B002` (passing out / causing out).** **Cue:** cleaving seed and pit introduces reciprocal living/dead extraction; `6:94` has reduced humans to singular created units, and `6:99` will unfold water-to-fruit emergence. **Role:** hard enclosures carry latent orientation and are split at the right threshold to release a different state. **Abductive move:** I infer that apparent deadness is a custody shell rather than proof of absent life; kernels, pits, splitting, and living output are supplied, while shell-as-custody is mine. **Reading change:** the biological image makes resurrection-like transition a recurring material operation.

2. **`ح ي ي` — `B003`/`B013` (animate being / life as saving benefit), `م و ت` — `B012` (lifeless stillness), and `ء ف ك` — `B001`/`B006` (turning from proper direction / perverted judgment).** **Cue:** living and dead are extracted in both directions under one agent, yet the audience is asked how it is turned; `6:96–99` multiply observable cycles. **Role:** reciprocal transformation destabilizes any fixed inference from present state to ultimate source or endpoint. **Abductive move:** I infer that ignoring process and absolutizing the visible state produces the epistemic turn away; transformations and question are supplied, while state-absolutization is mine. **Reading change:** `تُؤْفَكُونَ` becomes failure to follow the direction of a plainly reversible process.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:99` unfolded the kernel mechanism into a full branching production pipeline, confirming that dead-looking enclosure can custody successive living outputs.

## 6:96 — فَالِقُ ٱلْإِصْبَاحِ وَجَعَلَ ٱلَّيْلَ سَكَنًۭا وَٱلشَّمْسَ وَٱلْقَمَرَ حُسْبَانًۭا ۚ ذَٰلِكَ تَقْدِيرُ ٱلْعَزِيزِ ٱلْعَلِيمِ

### Activated readings

1. **`ف ل ق` — `B001`/`B002` (cleaving / daybreak splitting open), `ص ب ح` — `B001`/`B005` (morning opening / lamp), and `س ك ن` — `B001`/`B004` (cessation / object of rest).** **Cue:** dawn is split open after seed/pit in `6:95`, while night is deliberately made a resting state; `6:97` uses stars within darkness for travel. **Role:** cleaving initiates active visibility, and night closes motion into restorative settlement without becoming terminal death. **Abductive move:** I infer a calibrated oscillation between opening and rest; daybreak, night, and later navigation are supplied, while restorative cycle is mine. **Reading change:** the day-night pair is an engineered alternation of productive aperture and protected cessation.

2. **`ح س ب` — `B001`/`B006` (counting / accountable management), `ق د ر` — `B001`/`B005`/`B006` (extent / deliberative arrangement / fitting proportion), `ع ز ز` — `B001`/`B007` (might / hard cohesion), and `ع ل م` — `B001` (clear knowledge).** **Cue:** sun and moon are made a reckoning immediately before stars become navigation markers; the close attributes the system to measured arrangement, might, and knowledge. **Role:** celestial motion is a public clock whose stability depends on cohesive power and exact calibration. **Abductive move:** I infer that might here secures regularity rather than expressing disruption; reckoning, measure, might, and knowledge are supplied, while stability-as-use-of-might is mine. **Reading change:** cosmic power reads as reliable temporal infrastructure.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:97–98` showed what celestial regularity enables: stable markers support navigation, settlement, and entrusted placement, extending temporal infrastructure into lived orientation.

## 6:97 — وَهُوَ ٱلَّذِى جَعَلَ لَكُمُ ٱلنُّجُومَ لِتَهْتَدُوا۟ بِهَا فِى ظُلُمَٰتِ ٱلْبَرِّ وَٱلْبَحْرِ ۗ قَدْ فَصَّلْنَا ٱلْءَايَٰتِ لِقَوْمٍۢ يَعْلَمُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ن ج م` — `B001`/`B002`/`B006` (rising star / what rises into view / projecting marker), `ه د ي` — `B002` (intended direction), and `ظ ل م` — `B008` (withholding).** **Cue:** night was made rest in `6:96`, but stars convert its withholding darkness into navigable space across open land and sea; `6:98` next maps human settlement and deposit. **Role:** projecting lights supply stable reference points where the medium itself withholds orientation. **Abductive move:** I infer that guidance often works by installing relational markers rather than removing darkness; stars, darkness, and intended direction are supplied, while marker-instead-of-removal is mine. **Reading change:** darkness is not abolished but instrumented for travel.

2. **`ف ص ل` — `B005`/`B013` (joint between parts / clarifying detail), `ء ي ي` — `B002`/`B003` (directed mark / visible sign), and `ع ل م` — `B002` (distinguishing mark).** **Cue:** many stars become useful only when differentiated into a structured sign-system; sun/moon reckoning precedes and biological differentiation follows. **Role:** detailing supplies joints and distinctions that turn undifferentiated lights into a coordinate network. **Abductive move:** I infer that knowledge is relational pattern-recognition rather than noticing isolated brightness; stars, detailed signs, and knowing are supplied, while coordinate-network structure is mine. **Reading change:** the ayah links guidance to articulated multiplicity, not a singular spectacular sign.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:98–99` turned the stellar coordinate model into biological pattern-recognition, where one source generates differentiated placements and trajectories that detailed signs make legible.

## 6:98 — وَهُوَ ٱلَّذِىٓ أَنشَأَكُم مِّن نَّفْسٍۢ وَٰحِدَةٍۢ فَمُسْتَقَرٌّۭ وَمُسْتَوْدَعٌۭ ۗ قَدْ فَصَّلْنَا ٱلْءَايَٰتِ لِقَوْمٍۢ يَفْقَهُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ن ش ء` — `B001`/`B006` (rising and being raised / bringing into being), `ن ف س` — `B011` (living self), and `و ح د` — `B002`/`B005` (single unit / one in a shared respect).** **Cue:** humanity rises from one self after `6:94` returned each person to singularity and `6:95` released multiplicity from kernels; `6:99` repeats proliferating growth from one water source. **Role:** unity is a generative source-state from which differentiated persons emerge without losing shared reference. **Abductive move:** I infer that common origin and individual accountability are complementary scales; one self, generated plurality, and prior solitary return are supplied, while scale-complementarity is mine. **Reading change:** the verse holds species unity and personal singularity in one production model.

2. **`ق ر ر` — `B002`/`B003` (settled comfort / fixed residence) and `و د ع` — `B002`/`B005` (restful ease / entrusted deposit).** **Cue:** the two nominal states follow generation and precede plant stages; `6:94` has shown possessions left behind and `6:100–101` will reject invented kinship claims. **Role:** residence is a current settled phase, while deposit defines another phase by custodial transfer rather than ownership. **Abductive move:** I infer that human placement alternates between habitation and entrusted holding, neither being ultimate possession; the paired states are supplied, while temporal-custodial alternation is mine. **Reading change:** `مُسْتَقَرٌّ وَمُسْتَوْدَعٌ` maps life as successive custody states, not merely two locations.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:99–101` confirmed common origin without ownership: one water proliferates living forms, while every settled or deposited life remains inside total creation and knowledge.

## 6:99 — وَهُوَ ٱلَّذِىٓ أَنزَلَ مِنَ ٱلسَّمَآءِ مَآءًۭ فَأَخْرَجْنَا بِهِۦ نَبَاتَ كُلِّ شَىْءٍۢ فَأَخْرَجْنَا مِنْهُ خَضِرًۭا نُّخْرِجُ مِنْهُ حَبًّۭا مُّتَرَاكِبًۭا وَمِنَ ٱلنَّخْلِ مِن طَلْعِهَا قِنْوَانٌۭ دَانِيَةٌۭ وَجَنَّٰتٍۢ مِّنْ أَعْنَابٍۢ وَٱلزَّيْتُونَ وَٱلرُّمَّانَ مُشْتَبِهًۭا وَغَيْرَ مُتَشَٰبِهٍ ۗ ٱنظُرُوٓا۟ إِلَىٰ ثَمَرِهِۦٓ إِذَآ أَثْمَرَ وَيَنْعِهِۦٓ ۚ إِنَّ فِى ذَٰلِكُمْ لَءَايَٰتٍۢ لِّقَوْمٍۢ يُؤْمِنُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`م و ه` — `B001`/`B003` (water / supplying water), `خ ر ج` — `B002` (causing to come out), `ن ب ت` — `B001`/`B005` (plant emergence / stock or place of growth), `خ ض ر` — `B002` (fresh green growth), `ح ب ب` — `B001` (seed-kernel), and `ر ك ب` — `B002`/`B004` (superposition / composition).** **Cue:** `فَأَخْرَجْنَا`/`نُّخْرِجُ` recursively nests water, general growth, green tissue, and stacked grain; seed-splitting and human emergence precede in `6:95–98`. **Role:** one supplied input activates successive output layers, and composition stacks many kernels into organized yield. **Abductive move:** I infer a branching production pipeline in which each output becomes the containing input of the next stage; the repeated syntax and materials are supplied, while pipeline structure is mine. **Reading change:** the ayah reads as an explicit causal architecture of generative emergence.

2. **`ط ل ع` — `B005` (palm-spadix emergence), `ق ن و` — `B001`/`B004` (fitting together / date cluster), `د ن و` — `B001` (nearness), and `ج ن ن` — `B003` (tree-covered garden).** **Cue:** palm emergence yields fitted low clusters while other fruits occupy covering gardens; `6:100–101` contrasts such created relations with fabricated divine offspring. **Role:** reproductive forms become inspectably near and materially joined, displaying creation through structured dependence. **Abductive move:** I infer that the text lowers complex generation into the observer's reachable field as evidence; nearness, clusters, and command to look are supplied, while evidentiary lowering is mine. **Reading change:** abundance is not distant spectacle but accessible generative structure.

3. **`ش ب ه` — `B001`/`B002` (resemblance / ambiguity from similarity), `ث م ر` — `B001`/`B003` (ripe fruit / fruit of benefit), `ي ن ع` — `B001` (ripening), and `ن ظ ر` — `B001` (directing sight or insight).** **Cue:** similar and dissimilar forms are not left as a static classification; observers are told to watch fruit at initial fruiting and at maturity. **Role:** temporal observation resolves ambiguity by tracking how resemblance differentiates through development. **Abductive move:** I infer that signs reside in trajectories, not surfaces alone; similarity, fruiting, ripening, and looking are supplied, while trajectory-based discrimination is mine. **Reading change:** faith-producing sight compares stages and outcomes, preventing resemblance from flattening created difference.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:100–101` exposed why temporal observation matters: materially inspectable generation supplies the relational mechanics that fabricated divine offspring tears apart.

## 6:100 — وَجَعَلُوا۟ لِلَّهِ شُرَكَآءَ ٱلْجِنَّ وَخَلَقَهُمْ ۖ وَخَرَقُوا۟ لَهُۥ بَنِينَ وَبَنَٰتٍۭ بِغَيْرِ عِلْمٍۢ ۚ سُبْحَٰنَهُۥ وَتَعَٰلَىٰ عَمَّا يَصِفُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ج ع ل` — `B002` (turning something into a state), `ج ن ن` — `B005` (hidden spirit beings), `خ ل ق` — `B002` (bringing into being), and `ش ر ك` — `B001` (partnership).** **Cue:** they convert hidden created beings into partners immediately after `6:99` has exposed layered created emergence; `6:101` then distinguishes unprecedented origination from offspring. **Role:** social-conceptual making assigns a new status to beings whose actual ontological status is already supplied by creation. **Abductive move:** I infer that hiddenness provides room for status inflation because the created relation is less directly inspectable; hidden beings, createdness, and assigned partnership are supplied, while hiddenness-to-inflation is mine. **Reading change:** the error is a human state-conversion performed on creatures, not discovery of an unseen partnership.

2. **`خ ر ق` — `B001`/`B003`/`B004` (tearing / fabricating / lack of measured skill), `ب ن ي` — `B007` (filiation), and `ع ل م` — `B001` (clear knowledge).** **Cue:** sons and daughters are fabricated without knowledge between a detailed account of reproduction (`6:99`) and denial of any companion or child (`6:101`). **Role:** fabrication tears known generative relations from their material mechanism and clumsily stitches them onto God. **Abductive move:** I infer that invented genealogy reuses the vocabulary of observed creation while discarding its necessary relations; offspring language, absent knowledge, and adjacent reproductive detail are supplied, while torn-transfer is mine. **Reading change:** `خَرَقُوا۟` becomes a violent category splice, not only verbal invention.

3. **`س ب ح` — `B002` (declaring free and pure), `ع ل و` — `B001` (rising high), and `و ص ف` — `B001`/`B002` (showing by attributes / selling by description without sight).** **Cue:** purification and transcendence answer their descriptions; `6:103` soon denies visual capture while affirming subtle knowledge. **Role:** exaltation removes God from the descriptive market in which unseen reality is traded through unsupported attributes. **Abductive move:** I infer that their description claims inspection-equivalent knowledge they do not possess; unsupported genealogy and later visual asymmetry are supplied, while description-as-sale is an exploratory analogy. **Reading change:** the closing formula clears away not language itself but descriptions pretending to deliver an unseen object on human terms.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:101–103` made the category splice explicit: unprecedented origin, exhaustive creation, and asymmetrical perception leave no companion, offspring mechanism, or inspectable peer for the invented genealogy.

## 6:101 — بَدِيعُ ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ وَٱلْأَرْضِ ۖ أَنَّىٰ يَكُونُ لَهُۥ وَلَدٌۭ وَلَمْ تَكُن لَّهُۥ صَٰحِبَةٌۭ ۖ وَخَلَقَ كُلَّ شَىْءٍۢ ۖ وَهُوَ بِكُلِّ شَىْءٍ عَلِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **`ب د ع` — `B001`/`B003` (originating without precedent / wondrous novelty), `و ل د` — `B003`/`B005` (birth / generated or derived thing), and `ص ح ب` — `B001` (close companionship).** **Cue:** unprecedented origination is contrasted with offspring despite absence of the paired relation ordinary generation requires; `6:99–100` supplied observed reproductive stages and fabricated filiation. **Role:** derivation requires relational and temporal precedent, while origination establishes the entire field in which those relations occur. **Abductive move:** I infer a categorical incompatibility between being source of the system and being one generated node within it; origination, child, absent companion, and total creation are supplied, while system-versus-node is mine. **Reading change:** the question rejects offspring by generative mechanics, not merely assertion.

2. **`خ ل ق` — `B001`/`B002` (proportioning / originating), `ك ل ل` — `B003`/`B005` (totality / surrounding ring), `ش ي ء` — `B001` (knowable thing), and `ع ل م` — `B001` (knowing clearly).** **Cue:** “every thing” is repeated around creation and knowledge; `6:102` repeats total creation and trusteeship. **Role:** creation and knowledge form an exhaustive enclosure with no uncreated or unknown remainder available to become an independent relative. **Abductive move:** I infer that total coverage removes the ontological space in which a peer lineage could arise; repetition and totality are supplied, while no-remainder conclusion is mine. **Reading change:** the double totality functions as a closure argument against divine genealogy.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:102–103` closed every proposed remainder through total creation and trusteeship, then showed that the source contains sight rather than occupying sight's field as a generated node.

## 6:102 — ذَٰلِكُمُ ٱللَّهُ رَبُّكُمْ ۖ لَآ إِلَٰهَ إِلَّا هُوَ ۖ خَٰلِقُ كُلِّ شَىْءٍۢ فَٱعْبُدُوهُ ۚ وَهُوَ عَلَىٰ كُلِّ شَىْءٍۢ وَكِيلٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **`ر ب ب` — `B001`/`B002` (lordship / nurture and completion), `خ ل ق` — `B001`/`B002` (proportioning / originating), `ع ب د` — `B003` (worshipful obedience), and `و ك ل` — `B006` (one who undertakes, suffices, and guards).** **Cue:** exclusive deity, total creation, worship, and total trusteeship form a causal sequence; `6:103–104` then distinguish divine perception from human responsibility to see. **Role:** origination establishes dependence, nurture continues it, worship acknowledges it, and trusteeship maintains every dependent thing. **Abductive move:** I infer that the imperative follows from an ongoing creation-care relation rather than raw command; the four relations are supplied, while ongoing-dependence is mine. **Reading change:** worship is the creature's aligned response inside a total custodial system.

2. **`ك ل ل` — `B003`/`B005` (totality / surrounding ring) and `ش ي ء` — `B001` (knowable thing).** **Cue:** `كُلِّ شَىْءٍ` occurs on both sides of the command, first under creator and then under trustee. **Role:** the repeated totality encloses worship between origination and preservation, leaving no domain assigned to another god. **Abductive move:** I infer that exclusive worship is structurally secured by exhaustive beginning-and-continuance coverage; repetition is supplied, while the enclosure relation is mine. **Reading change:** monotheism is expressed as complete lifecycle custody rather than a bare numerical claim.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:103–104` clarified total custody as intimate but noncoercive: subtle knowledge encloses perception, while each self remains responsible for whether supplied insight becomes sight.

## 6:103 — لَّا تُدْرِكُهُ ٱلْأَبْصَٰرُ وَهُوَ يُدْرِكُ ٱلْأَبْصَٰرَ ۖ وَهُوَ ٱللَّطِيفُ ٱلْخَبِيرُ

### Activated readings

1. **`د ر ك` — `B001`/`B003` (reaching and catching / rope link or fastening loop) and `ب ص ر` — `B001`/`B002` (eye sight / inner sight).** **Cue:** identical verb and object reverse around the pronoun: sights cannot catch Him, but He catches sights; insights then arrive from the Lord in `6:104`. **Role:** perception tries to close a loop around its object, while divine apprehension encloses the perceiving faculty itself. **Abductive move:** I infer that the source of sight cannot be bounded as one item in sight's own field; reciprocal syntax and prior total trusteeship are supplied, while source-versus-field is mine. **Reading change:** invisibility is not reciprocal distance but asymmetric containment of the observer by the unseen knower.

2. **`ل ط ف` — `B001`/`B002` (gentle kindness / fine subtle hiddenness) and `خ ب ر` — `B001` (tested inner knowledge).** **Cue:** subtlety and interior knowledge explain the perceptual asymmetry; `6:104–105` show guidance arriving through insights and rotated signs rather than direct visual capture. **Role:** fineness permits intimate reach without object-like visibility, and tested knowledge penetrates the observer's interior. **Abductive move:** I infer that hidden mode is precisely how universal care can act at fine scale; divine subtlety, knowledge, and coming insights are supplied, while hiddenness-as-mode-of-care is mine. **Reading change:** `ٱللَّطِيفُ` corrects the equation of imperceptibility with absence.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:104–105` confirmed subtle hidden care as the mode of guidance: insights arrive and signs rotate through relations without making the divine source a visually capturable object.

## 6:104 — قَدْ جَآءَكُم بَصَآئِرُ مِن رَّبِّكُمْ ۖ فَمَنْ أَبْصَرَ فَلِنَفْسِهِۦ ۖ وَمَنْ عَمِىَ فَعَلَيْهَا ۚ وَمَآ أَنَا۠ عَلَيْكُم بِحَفِيظٍۢ

### Activated readings

1. **`ب ص ر` — `B002` (inner sight), `ن ف س` — `B010`/`B011` (precious value / living self), and `ع م ي` — `B002`/`B004` (blindness of insight / obscuring).** **Cue:** insights arrive collectively, but seeing or blindness returns to each self; `6:103` has distinguished the divine apprehender from human sight, and `6:105` rotates signs. **Role:** insight is supplied, but appropriation remains nontransferable; seeing preserves the self's value while self-obscuring loads consequence onto it. **Abductive move:** I infer that evidence can be public while perception remains an individually enacted relation; arriving insights and distributive clauses are supplied, while public-input/private-uptake is mine. **Reading change:** the ayah locates benefit and harm in the perceiver's processing, not scarcity of signs.

2. **`ح ف ظ` — `B001`/`B006` (preserving / keeping covenant) and `ب ص ر` — `B002` (inner sight).** **Cue:** the messenger disclaims guardianship immediately after assigning seeing to each self; `6:106–107` repeat following and non-custodianship. **Role:** a guardian could preserve another's relation, but inner sight cannot be kept by proxy. **Abductive move:** I infer that messenger delivery stops at the boundary of another person's perceptual covenant; insights, self-allocation, and non-guardianship are supplied, while boundary is mine. **Reading change:** the disclaimer protects the hearer's agency rather than diminishing the messenger's delivery.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:105–107` sharpened the agency boundary: public signs can be varied and faithfully followed, but no messenger can preserve another person's perceptual covenant by proxy.

## 6:105 — وَكَذَٰلِكَ نُصَرِّفُ ٱلْءَايَٰتِ وَلِيَقُولُوا۟ دَرَسْتَ وَلِنُبَيِّنَهُۥ لِقَوْمٍۢ يَعْلَمُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ص ر ف` — `B002`/`B003` (turning through states / resourceful maneuvering), `ء ي ي` — `B002`/`B003` (directed mark / visible sign), and `د ر س` — `B001`/`B002`/`B005` (effaced trace / repeated study / treading grain).** **Cue:** the same variation generates an accusation of prior study and clarification for knowers; `6:104` has split seeing from blindness, while `6:108` shows communities finding their works beautified. **Role:** repeated turning processes the sign like grain: it can release retained structure or wear the trace until it is attributed elsewhere. **Abductive move:** I infer that prior orientation determines whether repetition clarifies pattern or erases provenance; variation, two outputs, and differing knowers are supplied, while orientation-dependent processing is mine. **Reading change:** sign-rotation is a diagnostic operation that reveals the reader while revealing the sign.

2. **`ب ي ن` — `B004`/`B005` (becoming clear / disclosing meaning) and `ع ل م` — `B002` (distinguishing mark).** **Cue:** clarification is explicitly audience-specific, not universal in effect; `6:109–110` later show a promised sign failing against trained reversal. **Role:** knowledge supplies the distinctions needed to integrate varied signs into one disclosed relation. **Abductive move:** I infer that clarity is a constructed relation between articulated differences, not a brightness property carried by one sign; variation, clarification, and knowing people are supplied, while relational clarity is mine. **Reading change:** the verse explains why more signs can polarize rather than homogenize response.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:109–110` confirmed variation as diagnostic: even an utmost oath before a new sign cannot undo the heart-and-sight mechanism trained by the first refusal.

## 6:106 — ٱتَّبِعْ مَآ أُوحِىَ إِلَيْكَ مِن رَّبِّكَ ۖ لَآ إِلَٰهَ إِلَّا هُوَ ۖ وَأَعْرِضْ عَنِ ٱلْمُشْرِكِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ت ب ع` — `B003`/`B004` (stepwise tracking / ordered succession), `و ح ي` — `B001` (hidden imparting), and `ر ب ب` — `B002` (nurture and completion).** **Cue:** after signs generate polarized readings, the messenger is told to track only the transmitted sequence from his Lord; `6:107` removes responsibility for coercing partners. **Role:** following preserves ordered dependence on the nurturing source instead of chasing audience interpretation. **Abductive move:** I infer that the command stabilizes messenger action under interpretive turbulence; revelation, following, and divergent response are supplied, while stabilization is mine. **Reading change:** `ٱتَّبِعْ` is a process-control rule for the messenger, not only doctrinal obedience.

2. **`ع ر ض` — `B005` (turning one's side away) and `ش ر ك` — `B008` (divided or preoccupied mind).** **Cue:** this aversion is commanded alongside exclusive deity and is bounded by non-guardianship in `6:107`; insult is separately prohibited in `6:108`. **Role:** turning away prevents divided allegiance from capturing the messenger's operational attention without licensing aggression. **Abductive move:** I infer a disciplined disengagement that preserves route and speech ethics simultaneously; command, partnership, noncustody, and no-insult rule are supplied, while disciplined boundary is mine. **Reading change:** disengagement is neither contempt nor abandonment of delivery but protection from agenda division.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:107–108` bounded disengagement precisely: the messenger lacks coercive custody yet must avoid insult, preserving both operational focus and ethical address.

## 6:107 — وَلَوْ شَآءَ ٱللَّهُ مَآ أَشْرَكُوا۟ ۗ وَمَا جَعَلْنَٰكَ عَلَيْهِمْ حَفِيظًۭا ۖ وَمَآ أَنتَ عَلَيْهِم بِوَكِيلٍۢ

### Activated readings

1. **`ش ي ء` — the `B001` will variant, `ج ع ل` — `B002` (turning into a state), `ح ف ظ` — `B001`/`B003` (preserving / steady observance), and `و ك ل` — `B001`/`B006` (handing over / sufficient guardian).** **Cue:** divine willing could remove partnership, but the messenger has not been made either preserver or trustee over them; `6:108` leaves return and report to their Lord. **Role:** willing defines ultimate possibility, while nonappointment blocks the messenger from converting that possibility into coercive custody. **Abductive move:** I infer that divine capacity does not imply delegated human control; will, nonmaking, and role negations are supplied, while delegation distinction is mine. **Reading change:** the ayah simultaneously affirms sovereignty and protects the boundary of prophetic authority.

2. **`ش ر ك` — `B001`/`B008` (partnership / divided mind).** **Cue:** partnership is counterfactually removable by divine will but presently persists; the following no-insult rule treats its adherents as morally answerable interlocutors. **Role:** division remains a real enacted condition inside divine permission, not a force outside rule. **Abductive move:** I infer that permitted persistence creates the field in which accountable response and ethical address operate; persistence, noncoercion, and later return are supplied, while trial-field function is mine. **Reading change:** sovereignty does not flatten creaturely action into messenger-manageable behavior.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:108–109` showed what accountable persistence requires: noncoercion leaves interlocutors answerable, while sign custody remains divine despite their forceful demands.

## 6:108 — وَلَا تَسُبُّوا۟ ٱلَّذِينَ يَدْعُونَ مِن دُونِ ٱللَّهِ فَيَسُبُّوا۟ ٱللَّهَ عَدْوًۢا بِغَيْرِ عِلْمٍۢ ۗ كَذَٰلِكَ زَيَّنَّا لِكُلِّ أُمَّةٍ عَمَلَهُمْ ثُمَّ إِلَىٰ رَبِّهِم مَّرْجِعُهُمْ فَيُنَبِّئُهُم بِمَا كَانُوا۟ يَعْمَلُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`س ب ب` — `B001`/`B002`/`B003` (severing / insult / rope or means of connection) and `ع د و` — `B001`/`B006` (overstepping a bound / contagion and transmission).** **Cue:** one group's insult is predicted to trigger reciprocal insult against God; `6:106–107` command disengagement without custody, and `6:109` shows intensified oath-speech. **Role:** abusive speech is a connecting rope that transmits boundary violation across opposing communities even as it claims to sever from them. **Abductive move:** I infer a feedback loop in which provocation recruits the other party into mirroring the same act; insult, consequence clause, and ignorant aggression are supplied, while mimetic feedback is mine. **Reading change:** the prohibition is causal harm prevention, not etiquette detached from truth claims.

2. **`ز ي ن` — `B002` (making beauty show), `ء م م` — `B004`/`B005` (community / followed way), `ع م ل` — `B001`/`B005` (intentional work / social transaction), `ر ج ع` — `B001` (return), and `ن ب ء` — `B002` (reported news).** **Cue:** every community finds its own works attractive, then all return for an external report of those works. **Role:** internal beautification sustains group practice, while final report breaks the closed evaluative loop by supplying a source-independent account. **Abductive move:** I infer that insult strengthens the target group's defensive beautification and therefore impedes correction; adornment, community, work, and return are supplied, while insult-to-identity-hardening is mine. **Reading change:** ethical restraint serves epistemic correction by not feeding the community's self-confirming system.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:109–110` validated the feedback warning: intensified oath-speech does not repair reception and can sit atop a perceptual system already hardened by prior response.

## 6:109 — وَأَقْسَمُوا۟ بِٱللَّهِ جَهْدَ أَيْمَٰنِهِمْ لَئِن جَآءَتْهُمْ ءَايَةٌۭ لَّيُؤْمِنُنَّ بِهَا ۚ قُلْ إِنَّمَا ٱلْءَايَٰتُ عِندَ ٱللَّهِ ۖ وَمَا يُشْعِرُكُمْ أَنَّهَآ إِذَا جَآءَتْ لَا يُؤْمِنُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ق س م` — `B004`/`B006` (oath apportioned among holders / mind divided between courses), `ج ه د` — `B001`/`B003` (spending capacity under strain / pressing to strain), and `ي م ن` — `B003`/`B004` (oath by the right hand / rightful force).** **Cue:** the oath spends maximum verbal force promising belief, yet the close leaves nonbelief possible and `6:110` recalls the first refusal. **Role:** strained oath-performance attempts to compensate for an internally divided course by concentrating external force. **Abductive move:** I infer that intensity of commitment speech can mask rather than repair divided reception; oath, maximal effort, and predicted nonbelief are supplied, while compensation mechanism is mine. **Reading change:** “utmost oaths” become evidence of pressure, not reliable evidence of future uptake.

2. **`ء ي ي` — `B002`/`B003` (directed mark / visible sign), `ع ن د` — `B004` (presence and custody), and `ش ع ر` — `B004`/`B005` (skin-close inner garment / subtle awareness).** **Cue:** signs remain with God, while the audience lacks subtle awareness of how it would respond even if one arrived; `6:110–111` test increasingly direct evidence. **Role:** sign-custody controls arrival, but reception depends on an intimate inward sensitivity no oath can guarantee. **Abductive move:** I infer a separation between evidence provision and perceptual disposition; sign, custody, arrival, and possible nonbelief are supplied, while disposition distinction is mine. **Reading change:** the problem shifts from absent sign to unreadiness at the closest layer of the self.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:110–111` separated evidence provision from disposition decisively: reversed processing survives even maximal descent, speech, quantity, and face-to-face presence.

## 6:110 — وَنُقَلِّبُ أَفْـِٔدَتَهُمْ وَأَبْصَٰرَهُمْ كَمَا لَمْ يُؤْمِنُوا۟ بِهِۦٓ أَوَّلَ مَرَّةٍۢ وَنَذَرُهُمْ فِى طُغْيَٰنِهِمْ يَعْمَهُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ق ل ب` — `B004` (turning and reversing), `ف ء د` — `B001`/`B002` (kindling heat / heart as inner kindling), `ب ص ر` — `B001`/`B002` (sight / insight), and `م ر ر` — `B002` (repeated occurrence).** **Cue:** heart and sight are reversed in correspondence with the first nonbelief, after an oath that a new sign would work; `6:111` then shows even maximal external evidence failing. **Role:** first refusal kindles an inward orientation that reverses subsequent visual processing when the event repeats. **Abductive move:** I infer path dependence: an earlier response changes the mechanism that processes later input; first time, reversal, and later counterfactual evidence are supplied, while mechanism-learning is mine. **Reading change:** repeated unbelief is not a fresh neutral verdict but the output of a trained perceptual system.

2. **`ط غ ي` — `B001`/`B002` (overstepping / overflowing force), `ع م ه` — `B001` (bewildered wavering without guidance), and `و ذ ر` — `B003` (leaving be).** **Cue:** they are left inside their own overflowing transgression; `6:111–113` trace how embellished speech further attracts and satisfies the same hearts. **Role:** overstepping becomes an engulfing medium, and bewilderment is aimless motion within it rather than freedom outside restraint. **Abductive move:** I infer that being left allows the self-generated environment to feed back on perception; transgression, abandonment, and wavering are supplied, while environmental feedback is mine. **Reading change:** abandonment is exposure to the momentum of one's own overflow.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:111–113` filled out the self-generated environment: external evidence is saturated while an ornate counter-speech network warms, attracts, and satisfies the already turned heart.

## 6:111 — ۞ وَلَوْ أَنَّنَا نَزَّلْنَآ إِلَيْهِمُ ٱلْمَلَٰٓئِكَةَ وَكَلَّمَهُمُ ٱلْمَوْتَىٰ وَحَشَرْنَا عَلَيْهِمْ كُلَّ شَىْءٍۢ قُبُلًۭا مَّا كَانُوا۟ لِيُؤْمِنُوٓا۟ إِلَّآ أَن يَشَآءَ ٱللَّهُ وَلَٰكِنَّ أَكْثَرَهُمْ يَجْهَلُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ن ز ل` — `B002` (bringing down), `ك ل م` — `B001` (meaningful speech exchanged), `ح ش ر` — `B001` (gathering and driving), and `ق ب ل` — `B001`/`B009` (facing / mutually facing group).** **Cue:** angels descend, the dead speak, and every thing is gathered face-to-face, escalating the promised sign of `6:109`; the prior ayah has already located failure in reversed heart and sight. **Role:** the counterfactual saturates vertical arrival, temporal testimony, total quantity, and frontal proximity. **Abductive move:** I infer a deliberate exhaustion of external evidence dimensions; each mode is supplied, while treating them as an exhaustive matrix is mine. **Reading change:** persistent nonbelief is shown to survive not just one missing proof but maximal evidentiary co-presence.

2. **`ش ي ء` — the `B001` will variant, `ك ث ر` — `B003` (abundance in claims or people), and `ج ه ل` — `B003`/`B005` (unknown unmarked thing / agitation after stillness).** **Cue:** belief remains dependent on divine willing despite evidence abundance; `6:112–113` explain a competing speech network that attracts untrusting hearts. **Role:** ignorance is not lack of available objects but inability to mark and settle among them, leaving the majority agitated by abundance. **Abductive move:** I infer that more evidence cannot cure a broken distinguishing function and may give it more material to churn; total gathering, nonbelief, and ignorance are supplied, while overload is mine. **Reading change:** `أَكْثَرَهُمْ يَجْهَلُونَ` diagnoses disordered processing under abundance rather than informational poverty.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:112–113` explained maximal evidence failure through competing communication ecology, where hidden ornamented transmission supplies abundant material for disordered processing.

## 6:112 — وَكَذَٰلِكَ جَعَلْنَا لِكُلِّ نَبِىٍّ عَدُوًّۭا شَيَٰطِينَ ٱلْإِنسِ وَٱلْجِنِّ يُوحِى بَعْضُهُمْ إِلَىٰ بَعْضٍۢ زُخْرُفَ ٱلْقَوْلِ غُرُورًۭا ۚ وَلَوْ شَآءَ رَبُّكَ مَا فَعَلُوهُ ۖ فَذَرْهُمْ وَمَا يَفْتَرُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ع د و` — `B001`/`B003` (overstepping a bound / enemy), `ش ط ن` — `B001` (remoteness), `ء ن س` — `B001` (visible human presence), and `ج ن ن` — `B005` (hidden beings).** **Cue:** every prophet is assigned opposition spanning visible and hidden agents; `6:111` showed both angels and dead testimony as insufficient, while `6:113` locates the reception point in hearts. **Role:** enmity operates across the full visible-hidden social field, crossing proper boundaries to keep recipients remote from the prophetic address. **Abductive move:** I infer a distributed opposition network rather than one isolated antagonist; every prophet, human/hidden agents, and reciprocal transmission are supplied, while network structure is mine. **Reading change:** prophetic opposition becomes systemic communication ecology.

2. **`و ح ي` — `B001`/`B005` (hidden imparting / low hidden sound), `ب ع ض` — `B001` (partition), `ز خ ر ف` — `B001`/`B003` (ornament / ornate deceptive speech), `غ ر ر` — `B008` (misleading appearance), and `ف ر ي` — `B001`/`B003` (cutting and stitching / fabricating).** **Cue:** parts secretly transmit to parts an ornamented saying whose purpose is deception; `6:114` counters with a detailed descended book. **Role:** the network imitates revelation's hidden transmission while surface ornament conceals stitched provenance. **Abductive move:** I infer that counter-revelation gains force by mimicking both the channel and aesthetic coherence of true message; hidden transmission, ornament, and fabrication are supplied, while mimetic strategy is mine. **Reading change:** deception is infrastructural and stylistic, not simply false content.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:113–114` made the network's two levels visible: ornate speech inclines the heart and deposits conduct, while the detailed book counters it by exposing joints and provenance.

## 6:113 — وَلِتَصْغَىٰٓ إِلَيْهِ أَفْـِٔدَةُ ٱلَّذِينَ لَا يُؤْمِنُونَ بِٱلْءَاخِرَةِ وَلِيَرْضَوْهُ وَلِيَقْتَرِفُوا۟ مَا هُم مُّقْتَرِفُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ص غ و` — `B001`/`B002` (inclination of allegiance / turning the ear), `ف ء د` — `B001`/`B002` (kindling heat / inward heart), and `ر ض و` — `B001`/`B003` (acceptance / going along by consent).** **Cue:** ornate speech from `6:112` first attracts hearts, then receives approval; the next ayah offers a detailed book as judge. **Role:** ear-turn changes allegiance at the heated inward center, and repeated exposure settles as consent. **Abductive move:** I infer a staged persuasion path from attention to affective warming to approval; ornament, heart-inclination, and acceptance are supplied, while staging is mine. **Reading change:** nonbelief is maintained through a positive attraction process, not only resistance to proof.

2. **`ق ر ف` — `B001`/`B003`/`B005` (clinging rind / acquiring what clings / intimate contact).** **Cue:** the root repeats as purpose and accomplished identity after inclination and approval; `6:120` will use it again for earned sin. **Role:** approved speech crosses into conduct that coats the actor with a persistent residue. **Abductive move:** I infer that repeated acquisition feeds identity by depositing layers that then predispose further acquisition; duplicated wording and clinging branch are supplied, while feedback is mine. **Reading change:** `لِيَقْتَرِفُوا۟ مَا هُم مُّقْتَرِفُونَ` describes self-thickening habit, not a single isolated offense.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:114` showed the interruption point in staged persuasion: an articulated book can adjudicate the transition from attractive surface to consent before conduct becomes clinging identity.

## 6:114 — أَفَغَيْرَ ٱللَّهِ أَبْتَغِى حَكَمًۭا وَهُوَ ٱلَّذِىٓ أَنزَلَ إِلَيْكُمُ ٱلْكِتَٰبَ مُفَصَّلًۭا ۚ وَٱلَّذِينَ ءَاتَيْنَٰهُمُ ٱلْكِتَٰبَ يَعْلَمُونَ أَنَّهُۥ مُنَزَّلٌۭ مِّن رَّبِّكَ بِٱلْحَقِّ ۖ فَلَا تَكُونَنَّ مِنَ ٱلْمُمْتَرِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ح ك م` — `B001`/`B002` (restraining to set right / adjudication), `ك ت ب` — `B001`/`B002` (joining / written book), and `ف ص ل` — `B002`/`B005`/`B013` (separating truth / joints / detailing).** **Cue:** a judge is sought against the ornate counter-speech of `6:112–113`; the book is both joined and internally articulated, and `6:115` declares its word complete. **Role:** textual joints preserve the whole while separating claims, allowing the book to restrain deceptive mixture. **Abductive move:** I infer that detail is the book's adjudicative mechanism: it exposes where ornament had blurred boundaries; book, detail, and judge are supplied, while mechanism is mine. **Reading change:** the descended book judges by architecture as well as assertion.

2. **`ن ز ل` — `B002` (sending down), `ر ب ب` — `B003` (knowledge formed by care), `ح ق ق` — `B001`/`B010` (firm truth / tight-made firmness), and `م ر ي` — `B001`/`B004` (drawing out by rubbing / doubt).** **Cue:** prior book-recipients know the source and firmness of descent; the addressee is told not to enter doubt after an ayah on heart-tilting persuasion. **Role:** known provenance anchors the book, while doubt is friction that could keep working against that settled joint structure. **Abductive move:** I infer that social counter-speech tries to rub a complete source-chain back into uncertainty; knowledge, descent, truth, and warning against doubt are supplied, while friction is mine. **Reading change:** `مُمْتَرِينَ` names an active destabilization process, not a momentary question.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:115–116` confirmed the book's architectural judgment: completed truth and justice form a stable weave against the majority's conjectural measurements.

## 6:115 — وَتَمَّتْ كَلِمَتُ رَبِّكَ صِدْقًۭا وَعَدْلًۭا ۚ لَّا مُبَدِّلَ لِكَلِمَٰتِهِۦ ۚ وَهُوَ ٱلسَّمِيعُ ٱلْعَلِيمُ

### Activated readings

1. **`ت م م` — `B001`/`B007` (full completion / the piece completing a weave), `ك ل م` — `B001`/`B002` (meaningful speech / word), `ص د ق` — `B001`/`B002` (truth / solidity and straightness), and `ع د ل` — `B001`/`B005` (even justice / straightening).** **Cue:** the detailed book of `6:114` is followed by completed word, while `6:116` contrasts majority conjecture and rough estimate. **Role:** truth supplies tensile solidity and justice supplies balanced alignment, completing the weave without missing piece. **Abductive move:** I infer that completeness is structural: factual and normative strands mutually stabilize the message; completion, truth, and justice are supplied, while woven interdependence is mine. **Reading change:** the word is complete because it is both straight in report and balanced in demand.

2. **`ب د ل` — `B001`/`B002` (replacement / alteration), `س م ع` — `B003` (understanding and compliance), and `ع ل م` — `B001` (clear knowledge).** **Cue:** no one can replace the words, and divine hearing/knowledge close the statement before human majority speech is rejected. **Role:** complete reception and knowledge secure the word against pressure to revise it around unheard cases. **Abductive move:** I infer that immutability is compatible with responsiveness because no voice or condition escapes the speaker's awareness; nonreplacement, hearing, and knowledge are supplied, while compatibility is mine. **Reading change:** fixed words do not imply informational rigidity; they issue from exhaustive reception.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:116–117` showed why exhaustive hearing and knowledge secure immutable words: crowd pressure and guessed measures cannot replace trajectory-level discrimination.

## 6:116 — وَإِن تُطِعْ أَكْثَرَ مَن فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ يُضِلُّوكَ عَن سَبِيلِ ٱللَّهِ ۚ إِن يَتَّبِعُونَ إِلَّا ٱلظَّنَّ وَإِنْ هُمْ إِلَّا يَخْرُصُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ط و ع` — `B001` (pliant obedience), `ك ث ر` — `B002`/`B003` (outnumbering / abundance in claims), `ض ل ل` — `B001` (straying from an aim), and `س ب ل` — `B001` (extended traversed way).** **Cue:** majority obedience is placed against the completed word of `6:115`; `6:117` says divine knowledge, not count, distinguishes paths. **Role:** social abundance exerts pliant pressure that can bend a traveler off the extended divine course. **Abductive move:** I infer that numerical scale becomes navigational force when converted into obedience; majority, obedience, and misdirection are supplied, while count-to-force conversion is mine. **Reading change:** the warning targets crowd-induced route deformation rather than consultation as such.

2. **`ت ب ع` — `B003` (stepwise tracking), `ظ ن ن` — `B002`/`B003` (expected sign / uncertain supposition), and `خ ر ص` — `B001`/`B002` (conjectural measure / speaking without knowledge).** **Cue:** the majority themselves follow only supposition and estimate; `6:119` will contrast detailed bounds with desire-led misguidance. **Role:** a weak expected sign is converted into a guessed measure, then repeated tracking turns the estimate into a road others are asked to obey. **Abductive move:** I infer a claim-production chain from ambiguous indication to measurement to social direction; each lexical stage is supplied, while the causal chain is mine. **Reading change:** error becomes industrially reproducible despite lacking firm source data.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:117–119` corrected count-based authority with practical route marks, as divine discrimination becomes detailed provenance and necessity-sensitive food boundaries.

## 6:117 — إِنَّ رَبَّكَ هُوَ أَعْلَمُ مَن يَضِلُّ عَن سَبِيلِهِۦ ۖ وَهُوَ أَعْلَمُ بِٱلْمُهْتَدِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ر ب ب` — `B003` (knowledge formed through care), `ع ل م` — `B002` (distinguishing mark), `ض ل ل` — `B001`/`B003` (straying / losing location), `س ب ل` — `B001` (traversed way), and `ه د ي` — `B002` (intended direction).** **Cue:** doubled superlative knowledge answers majority conjecture, then practical food distinctions follow in `6:118–121`. **Role:** nurturant knowledge reads route-marks that reveal both departure and maintained direction. **Abductive move:** I infer that classification rests on observed trajectory rather than declared group size; straying/guided persons, path, and superior knowledge are supplied, while trajectory-based classification is mine. **Reading change:** divine knowing is active path-discrimination that grounds the coming conduct rules.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:118–121` enacted nurturant path-discrimination at the bodily level, distinguishing source-aware ingestion and calibrated necessity from desire-led boundary crossing.

## 6:118 — فَكُلُوا۟ مِمَّا ذُكِرَ ٱسْمُ ٱللَّهِ عَلَيْهِ إِن كُنتُم بِـَٔايَٰتِهِۦ مُؤْمِنِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ء ك ل` — `B001`/`B003` (ingesting food / consumable provision), `ذ ك ر` — `B004`/`B009` (mention / reminder), and `س م و` — `B005` (name as raised designation).** **Cue:** eating follows path-discrimination and is conditioned on belief in signs; `6:119` adds detailed protected bounds and necessity. **Role:** naming raises the provision's source into awareness at the moment material passes into the body. **Abductive move:** I infer that mention integrates bodily dependence with the acknowledged sign-system; ingestion, name, and belief condition are supplied, while integration is mine. **Reading change:** the rule makes eating an epistemic act in which provision is internalized under its true provenance.

2. **`ء م ن` — `B001`/`B002` (settled trust / confirming assent) and `ء ي ي` — `B002`/`B003` (directed mark / visible sign).** **Cue:** the imperative is addressed conditionally to sign-trusters, and the next ayat distinguish divine detail from desire-based argument. **Role:** food practice becomes a visible mark that tests whether assent has settled into ordinary dependence. **Abductive move:** I infer that repeated intake operationalizes belief at a high-frequency bodily boundary; belief, signs, and eating are supplied, while frequency-and-boundary are mine. **Reading change:** faith is measured in mundane incorporation, not confined to verbal affirmation.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:119–121` sharpened ordinary eating into a diagnostic practice: detailed exception protects life, while omission of the divine name exposes the competing hidden allegiance.

## 6:119 — وَمَا لَكُمْ أَلَّا تَأْكُلُوا۟ مِمَّا ذُكِرَ ٱسْمُ ٱللَّهِ عَلَيْهِ وَقَدْ فَصَّلَ لَكُم مَّا حَرَّمَ عَلَيْكُمْ إِلَّا مَا ٱضْطُرِرْتُمْ إِلَيْهِ ۗ وَإِنَّ كَثِيرًۭا لَّيُضِلُّونَ بِأَهْوَآئِهِم بِغَيْرِ عِلْمٍ ۗ إِنَّ رَبَّكَ هُوَ أَعْلَمُ بِٱلْمُعْتَدِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ف ص ل` — `B005`/`B013` (joint / clarifying detail), `ح ر م` — `B003`/`B006` (protected precinct / inviolable claim), and `ض ر ر` — `B003` (compulsion under necessity).** **Cue:** forbidden items have been detailed, yet emergency is explicitly jointed into the boundary as exception; `6:115` paired truth and justice, while `6:121` guards the other side of the rule. **Role:** sacred limits protect rather than indiscriminately constrict, and necessity is a designed articulation preserving life under pressure. **Abductive move:** I infer that a just boundary includes its own emergency valve; detail, prohibition, and exception are supplied, while protective design is mine. **Reading change:** the exception is evidence of the rule's calibrated integrity, not erosion of it.

2. **`ك ث ر` — `B003` (abundance in claims), `ه و ي` — `B002`/`B004` (falling / desire), `ع د و` — `B001` (overstepping a bound), and `ع ل م` — `B002` (distinguishing mark).** **Cue:** many mislead without knowledge through desires, immediately after exact boundaries and exception are supplied; the Lord knows transgressors. **Role:** desire tilts interpretation, abundant repetition socializes it, and transgression crosses a marked limit. **Abductive move:** I infer that counterfeit rulings imitate flexibility while being driven by appetite rather than necessity; desire, misguidance, ignorance, and the genuine exception are supplied, while counterfeit-flexibility is mine. **Reading change:** the ayah distinguishes humane exception from desire-led boundary manipulation.

### Retrospective surprises
