Later `6:120–121` confirmed the boundary's integrity on both surfaces: outward food practice must align with inward acquisition, and desire cannot turn a protective exception into unauthorized flexibility.

## 6:120 — وَذَرُوا۟ ظَٰهِرَ ٱلْإِثْمِ وَبَاطِنَهُۥٓ ۚ إِنَّ ٱلَّذِينَ يَكْسِبُونَ ٱلْإِثْمَ سَيُجْزَوْنَ بِمَا كَانُوا۟ يَقْتَرِفُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ظ ه ر` — `B001`/`B003` (coming into view / outer surface), `ب ط ن` — `B001`/`B002` (inside / hidden inward reality), and `ء ث م` — `B001`/`B004` (lagging from good / loaded sin).** **Cue:** outer and inner sin are paired between public food boundaries and hidden counter-revelation in `6:121`; `6:122` contrasts a person walking in light with enclosure in darkness. **Role:** sin operates across surface and interior, slowing the whole agent and loading both visible action and hidden motive. **Abductive move:** I infer that regulating only public ingestion leaves the inward acquisition channel untouched; food rules, inner/outer pair, and covert persuasion are supplied, while dual-channel warning is mine. **Reading change:** the command prevents ritual surface-compliance from concealing interior lag.

2. **`ك س ب` — `B001` (seeking and obtaining), `ق ر ف` — `B001`/`B003` (clinging rind / acquiring what clings), and `ج ز ي` — `B001` (matching recompense).** **Cue:** acquisition vocabulary doubles around sin and what clings to the actor; `6:113` used the same clinging root for conduct generated by ornate speech. **Role:** earning brings sin into possession, while repeated contact coats the earner with a durable layer matched by recompense. **Abductive move:** I infer that consequence tracks adhesion to the person rather than an external list of isolated acts; earning, clinging, and matching return are supplied, while adhesion model is mine. **Reading change:** hidden and visible offenses become a material accretion the agent carries into recompense.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:121–122` confirmed the dual-channel warning: covert counter-revelation governs intake from within, while revival or darkness determines whether the resulting coating becomes navigable public conduct.

## 6:121 — وَلَا تَأْكُلُوا۟ مِمَّا لَمْ يُذْكَرِ ٱسْمُ ٱللَّهِ عَلَيْهِ وَإِنَّهُۥ لَفِسْقٌۭ ۗ وَإِنَّ ٱلشَّيَٰطِينَ لَيُوحُونَ إِلَىٰٓ أَوْلِيَآئِهِمْ لِيُجَٰدِلُوكُمْ ۖ وَإِنْ أَطَعْتُمُوهُمْ إِنَّكُمْ لَمُشْرِكُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ء ك ل` — `B001`/`B003` (ingestion / provision), `س م و` — `B005` (name as raised designation), `ذ ك ر` — `B004` (mention), and `ف س ق` — `B002` (a date emerging from its peel).** **Cue:** this rule mirrors `6:118`, but omitted designation now marks boundary-exit; `6:122` will contrast light-enabled walking with inability to exit darkness. **Role:** naming keeps provision inside an acknowledged source relation, while *fisq* images emergence beyond its protective skin. **Abductive move:** I infer that omission is not empty silence but removal of the relational layer governing intake; name, ingestion, and emergence are supplied, while relational-peel is mine. **Reading change:** prohibited eating enacts a provenance rupture at the body's boundary.

2. **`ش ط ن` — `B001` (remoteness), `و ح ي` — `B001` (hidden imparting), `و ل ي` — `B001`/`B004` (nearness / alliance), `ج د ل` — `B001`/`B002` (twisting / disputation), `ط و ع` — `B001` (obedience), and `ش ر ك` — `B001` (partnership).** **Cue:** remote agents secretly transmit to close allies, who twist the food rule in debate; obedience to that argument is declared partnership. **Role:** the chain converts hidden message into social proximity, argumentative twisting, and finally enacted allegiance. **Abductive move:** I infer that partnership occurs at the point external counter-revelation becomes the rule one obeys; transmission, alliance, dispute, obedience, and conclusion are supplied, while conversion point is mine. **Reading change:** the dietary debate is revealed as a governance contest over whose communication chain directs conduct.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:122–123` scaled the governance contest outward, as hidden allegiance becomes either light-bearing social movement or elite plotting that directs an entire settlement.

## 6:122 — أَوَمَن كَانَ مَيْتًۭا فَأَحْيَيْنَٰهُ وَجَعَلْنَا لَهُۥ نُورًۭا يَمْشِى بِهِۦ فِى ٱلنَّاسِ كَمَن مَّثَلُهُۥ فِى ٱلظُّلُمَٰتِ لَيْسَ بِخَارِجٍۢ مِّنْهَا ۚ كَذَٰلِكَ زُيِّنَ لِلْكَٰفِرِينَ مَا كَانُوا۟ يَعْمَلُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`م و ت` — `B006`/`B012` (deadness of heart / lifeless stillness), `ح ي ي` — `B013` (life as saving benefit), `ن و ر` — `B005` (beacon or landmark), and `م ش ي` — `B001`/`B005` (walking / an effect spreading within).** **Cue:** life is immediately operationalized as a light carried while walking among people; `6:121` showed hidden guidance chains directing conduct, and `6:123` moves to settlement-scale influence. **Role:** revival restores responsive motion, while portable light supplies and spreads orientation through the social field. **Abductive move:** I infer that spiritual life is measured by navigable movement and its transmitted effect; life, light, walking, and people are supplied, while social transmission is mine. **Reading change:** revival is not an inward state alone but public mobility under a reliable landmark.

2. **`ظ ل م` — `B008` (withholding), `خ ر ج` — `B001` (passing out from within), `ز ي ن` — `B002` (making beauty show), and `ك ف ر` — `B001` (covering).** **Cue:** the comparison figure cannot exit darkness, yet rejecters find their works beautified; `6:123–124` show those works as elite plotting and status demand. **Role:** darkness withholds an exit, while adornment makes the interior enclosure appear sufficient and covering keeps its boundary intact. **Abductive move:** I infer that beautification neutralizes escape desire by making captivity attractive; no exit, adorned work, and covering are supplied, while desire-neutralization is mine. **Reading change:** the person remains in darkness not only from absent light but from a feedback system that decorates nonexit.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:123–124` made both sides of the comparison structural: portable light spreads among people, while decorated nonexit concentrates into elite plotting and status demands.

## 6:123 — وَكَذَٰلِكَ جَعَلْنَا فِى كُلِّ قَرْيَةٍ أَكَٰبِرَ مُجْرِمِيهَا لِيَمْكُرُوا۟ فِيهَا ۖ وَمَا يَمْكُرُونَ إِلَّا بِأَنفُسِهِمْ وَمَا يَشْعُرُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ق ر ي` — `B001`/`B006` (people gathered in settlement / common course), `ك ب ر` — `B005`/`B006` (rank and leadership / self-magnification), and `ج ر م` — `B003`/`B004` (earning and causing to incur / guilt).** **Cue:** every settlement receives high-ranked criminals, after `6:122` contrasted socially walking light with beautified work; `6:124` makes their rank-hunger explicit. **Role:** concentrated rank turns private acquisition into a city-wide course, while criminal earning incurs a cost even as it shapes others. **Abductive move:** I infer that elite position amplifies conduct through the settlement's shared channels; village, magnates, crime, and next-ayah demand are supplied, while amplification is mine. **Reading change:** the ayah describes structural concentration of misguidance, not merely the presence of bad individuals.

2. **`م ك ر` — `B004` (ochre dye or pigment), `ن ف س` — `B013` (hidden intent), and `ش ع ر` — `B004`/`B005` (skin-close inner garment / subtle awareness).** **Cue:** the root repeats around conduct that affects only their own selves without their awareness; beautification precedes in `6:122`, and abasement follows in `6:124`. **Role:** the pigment branch supplies an exploratory image for strategy that colors the public surface while staining the agent's own interior. **Abductive move:** I infer that the social disguise rebounds because its repeated production trains and marks the producer; self-directed effect and unawareness are supplied, while dye-rebound is mine. **Reading change:** plotting becomes a coloring operation whose least perceived but most durable target is the plotter.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:124` exposed the plotters' hidden target as rank itself, and the promised abasement confirmed the dye-rebound image as a reversal of manufactured elevation.

## 6:124 — وَإِذَا جَآءَتْهُمْ ءَايَةٌۭ قَالُوا۟ لَن نُّؤْمِنَ حَتَّىٰ نُؤْتَىٰ مِثْلَ مَآ أُوتِىَ رُسُلُ ٱللَّهِ ۘ ٱللَّهُ أَعْلَمُ حَيْثُ يَجْعَلُ رِسَالَتَهُۥ ۗ سَيُصِيبُ ٱلَّذِينَ أَجْرَمُوا۟ صَغَارٌ عِندَ ٱللَّهِ وَعَذَابٌۭ شَدِيدٌۢ بِمَا كَانُوا۟ يَمْكُرُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`م ث ل` — `B001` (likeness and counterpart), `ء ت ي` — `B002` (granting), `ح ي ث` — `B001` (indeterminate place), `ج ع ل` — `B002` (placing into a state), and `ر س ل` — `B002` (messenger and message).** **Cue:** a sign's arrival triggers a demand for equal grant and messenger status; the reply relocates placement to divine knowledge, while `6:123` identified high-ranked claimants. **Role:** likeness becomes a status prerequisite invented by recipients, but message-placement follows source knowledge rather than social symmetry. **Abductive move:** I infer that they condition truth on being elevated to its delivery rank, converting reception into competition; sign, equal grant, elite context, and placement are supplied, while status-competition is mine. **Reading change:** their nonbelief is exposed as refusal of unequal role distribution rather than lack of evidence.

2. **`ص و ب` — `B003`/`B004` (aiming and hitting / affliction that hits), `ج ر م` — `B003` (causing to incur), `ص غ ر` — `B002` (abasement), and `ش د د` — `B001`/`B002` (fastened bond / intense hardness).** **Cue:** those who target social status are themselves to be hit by abasement and tightly severe punishment; the cause given is their plotting. **Role:** affliction's aim reverses the elite's strategic targeting, while abasement inverts demanded elevation. **Abductive move:** I infer a directional and scalar correspondence between attempted rank capture and resulting lowliness; demand, plotting, hitting, and abasement are supplied, while correspondence is mine. **Reading change:** consequence is a precise reversal of the plot's social geometry.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:125` internalized the status geometry: the demand to be raised like messengers returns as constricted upward striving that makes reception itself difficult to breathe.

## 6:125 — فَمَن يُرِدِ ٱللَّهُ أَن يَهْدِيَهُۥ يَشْرَحْ صَدْرَهُۥ لِلْإِسْلَٰمِ ۖ وَمَن يُرِدْ أَن يُضِلَّهُۥ يَجْعَلْ صَدْرَهُۥ ضَيِّقًا حَرَجًۭا كَأَنَّمَا يَصَّعَّدُ فِى ٱلسَّمَآءِ ۚ كَذَٰلِكَ يَجْعَلُ ٱللَّهُ ٱلرِّجْسَ عَلَى ٱلَّذِينَ لَا يُؤْمِنُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ش ر ح` — `B001`/`B005` (opening meaning / expansive inclination), `ص د ر` — `B001`/`B004` (chest / source from which forms issue), `س ل م` — `B001`/`B012` (soundness / handing over), and `ه د ي` — `B002` (intended direction).** **Cue:** guidance is described as opening the chest toward surrender after status competition in `6:124`; `6:126` names the result a straight path. **Role:** opening gives meaning and inclination room to issue from the bodily front, while surrender releases resistance into a sound direction. **Abductive move:** I infer that guidance changes processing capacity before supplying more content; chest-opening, Islam, and path are supplied, while capacity-first ordering is mine. **Reading change:** guidance is embodied aperture that makes the course respirable and intelligible.

2. **`ض ي ق` — `B001`/`B002` (narrowness / chest distress), `ح ر ج` — `B001`/`B002` (dense entanglement / constriction), `ص ع د` — `B003`/`B007` (arduous ascent / upward breath), and `س م و` — `B004` (overhead sky).** **Cue:** misguidance compresses the chest like impossible ascent; the preceding ayah sought upward rank, while the following ayah offers a level straight route. **Role:** narrowness reduces internal volume, entanglement blocks passage, and ascent makes each breath costly. **Abductive move:** I infer that status-driven upward striving is repurposed as an image of the receiver's self-created respiratory burden; rank demand, ascent, and constriction are supplied, while the status link is mine. **Reading change:** misguidance becomes a lived mechanics of diminishing capacity, not mere wrong coordinates.

3. **`ر ج س` — `B001`/`B005` (tainting defilement / confused entanglement) and `ء م ن` — `B001` (settled trust).** **Cue:** taint is placed on those who do not trust after bodily entanglement is described; `6:127` contrasts their state with the home of peace. **Role:** defilement is the residue and further tangle produced by failed settlement. **Abductive move:** I infer a feedback loop in which constricted reception generates contaminating confusion that makes trust still harder; constriction, taint, and nonbelief are supplied, while feedback is mine. **Reading change:** `ٱلرِّجْسَ` is an accumulating process-state rather than a detached label.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:126–127` confirmed capacity-first guidance, because the opened chest enters a sustaining straight passage and reaches a peaceful dwelling rather than performing an unaided ascent.

## 6:126 — وَهَٰذَا صِرَٰطُ رَبِّكَ مُسْتَقِيمًۭا ۗ قَدْ فَصَّلْنَا ٱلْءَايَٰتِ لِقَوْمٍۢ يَذَّكَّرُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ص ر ط` — `B001`/`B002` (straight road / disappearing in passage), `ق و م` — `B008` (straightness), and `ر ب ب` — `B002` (nurture and completion).** **Cue:** the straight road directly answers the constricted upward struggle of `6:125` and leads into the peaceful dwelling of `6:127`. **Role:** the road absorbs the traveler into a level passage whose nurturant structure keeps correcting direction. **Abductive move:** I infer that straightness is an environmental property of the route as well as a demand on the traveler; road, qualification, Lord, and destination are supplied, while route-supported correction is mine. **Reading change:** guidance offers a sustaining passage, not another unaided climb.

2. **`ف ص ل` — `B005`/`B013` (joint / clarifying detail), `ء ي ي` — `B002`/`B003` (directed mark / visible sign), and `ذ ك ر` — `B003`/`B009` (bringing to mind / reminder).** **Cue:** detailed signs are addressed to people who remember, after chest-opening and before final dwelling; `6:130` later says messengers sequentially recounted those signs. **Role:** articulated markers let memory repeatedly reconstruct the route at its joints. **Abductive move:** I infer that remembrance is active route maintenance, not storage of isolated statements; detail, signs, and remembering are supplied, while reconstruction is mine. **Reading change:** the ayah links textual articulation to durable navigation.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:127` made route-supported correction terminally visible in the home of peace, while `6:130` showed messenger recitation supplying the joints memory must reconstruct.

## 6:127 — ۞ لَهُمْ دَارُ ٱلسَّلَٰمِ عِندَ رَبِّهِمْ ۖ وَهُوَ وَلِيُّهُم بِمَا كَانُوا۟ يَعْمَلُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`د و ر` — `B001`/`B003` (encircling / circulation of states), `س ل م` — `B001`/`B004` (sound safety / peace), and `ع ن د` — `B004` (proximity and presence).** **Cue:** the straight path of `6:126` terminates in a house near the Lord, while `6:128` contrasts a fire-lodging built from mutual exploitation. **Role:** the dwelling encloses circulating states within settled peace and locates that enclosure at protected proximity. **Abductive move:** I infer that the destination stabilizes the traveler's previously changing states without erasing relational nearness; path, house, peace, and proximity are supplied, while stabilization is mine. **Reading change:** the home of peace is the settled topology of guidance, not merely a reward location.

2. **`و ل ي` — `B001`/`B003`/`B004` (nearness / charge / loyal support) and `ع م ل` — `B001` (intentional work).** **Cue:** divine patronage is linked `بِمَا` to their work; the next two ayat show corrupt allies produced through reciprocal benefit and earnings. **Role:** work operationalizes a direction that results in either divine nearness or mutually corrupt alliance. **Abductive move:** I infer that repeated action selects and thickens the patronage environment one inhabits; work and patronage are supplied, while environment-selection is mine. **Reading change:** `وَلِيُّهُم` is the mature relational outcome of a practiced course.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:128–129` supplied the inverse patronage environment: reciprocal enjoyment matures into fire-lodging and wrongdoers become one another's assigned allies through their earnings.

## 6:128 — وَيَوْمَ يَحْشُرُهُمْ جَمِيعًۭا يَٰمَعْشَرَ ٱلْجِنِّ قَدِ ٱسْتَكْثَرْتُم مِّنَ ٱلْإِنسِ ۖ وَقَالَ أَوْلِيَآؤُهُم مِّنَ ٱلْإِنسِ رَبَّنَا ٱسْتَمْتَعَ بَعْضُنَا بِبَعْضٍۢ وَبَلَغْنَآ أَجَلَنَا ٱلَّذِىٓ أَجَّلْتَ لَنَا ۚ قَالَ ٱلنَّارُ مَثْوَىٰكُمْ خَٰلِدِينَ فِيهَآ إِلَّا مَا شَآءَ ٱللَّهُ ۗ إِنَّ رَبَّكَ حَكِيمٌ عَلِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **`ع ش ر` — `B012`/`B013` (intermingling companionship / kin group or community), `ج ن ن` — `B005` (hidden beings), `ء ن س` — `B001`/`B003` (visible humans / familiar comfort), `ك ث ر` — `B001`/`B003` (increase / abundance attached to a party), and `و ل ي` — `B004` (alliance).** **Cue:** total gathering reveals a hidden-visible alliance that had expanded through familiarity; `6:129` says wrongdoers are assigned to one another by earnings. **Role:** companionship makes cross-domain influence familiar, familiarity recruits more humans, and alliance stabilizes the growing network. **Abductive move:** I infer an adoption loop in which each new benefit normalizes and expands the relation; increase, allies, mutual enjoyment, and later reciprocal assignment are supplied, while adoption loop is mine. **Reading change:** “you took many humans” reads as network scaling through intimate utility, not simple headcount.

2. **`م ت ع` — `B001`/`B007` (enjoyed benefit / prolonged keeping for benefit), `ب ع ض` — `B001` (parts), `ب ل غ` — `B001` (reaching the limit), and `ء ج ل` — `B001` (fixed term).** **Cue:** both parts confess reciprocal use until the appointed boundary is reached; temporary benefit is followed by fire as lodging. **Role:** exchange prolongs the alliance but cannot extend its externally fixed term. **Abductive move:** I infer that mutual utility disguises finitude by making continuation feel self-sustaining; enjoyment, reciprocity, and reached term are supplied, while disguise is mine. **Reading change:** the confession exposes pleasure as the mechanism that kept a bounded arrangement operating until closure.

3. **`ث و ي` — `B001`/`B002` (staying / lodging-place), `خ ل د` — `B001`/`B002` (enduring permanence / clinging and staying close), `ن و ر` — `B002` (burning fire), and `ش ي ء` — the `B001` will variant.** **Cue:** the peaceful home of `6:127` is replaced by fire-lodging, with permanence still placed under divine willing. **Role:** what had been elective alliance becomes compulsory residence and clinging proximity to its consequence. **Abductive move:** I infer that practiced nearness matures into a matching habitation while final duration remains under sovereignty; alliance, lodging, permanence, and exception are supplied, while maturation is mine. **Reading change:** fire is the settled relational habitat produced by the prior network, not a disconnected destination.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:129` confirmed network scaling as assortative formation, where repeatedly acquired benefit sorts compatible parties into mutually reinforcing proximity and authority.

## 6:129 — وَكَذَٰلِكَ نُوَلِّى بَعْضَ ٱلظَّٰلِمِينَ بَعْضًۢا بِمَا كَانُوا۟ يَكْسِبُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`و ل ي` — `B001`/`B003`/`B004` (nearness / authority / alliance), `ب ع ض` — `B001` (partition into portions), `ظ ل م` — `B004`/`B008` (misplacing / withholding), and `ك س ب` — `B001` (seeking and obtaining).** **Cue:** the prior ayah exposed reciprocal allies, and the next ayah audits whether messengers reached them; this ayah attributes mutual assignment to earnings. **Role:** acquired conduct sorts wrongdoers into neighboring portions whose authority and support reinforce the same misplacement. **Abductive move:** I infer assortative social formation: repeated choices make compatible wrongdoers available as one another's environment; alliance, parts, and earning are supplied, while sorting mechanism is mine. **Reading change:** corrupt patronage is both consequence and accelerator of acquired conduct.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:130` restored individual self-testimony inside the corrupt alliance, showing that a socially assigned environment accelerates conduct without transferring its ownership.

## 6:130 — يَٰمَعْشَرَ ٱلْجِنِّ وَٱلْإِنسِ أَلَمْ يَأْتِكُمْ رُسُلٌۭ مِّنكُمْ يَقُصُّونَ عَلَيْكُمْ ءَايَٰتِى وَيُنذِرُونَكُمْ لِقَآءَ يَوْمِكُمْ هَٰذَا ۚ قَالُوا۟ شَهِدْنَا عَلَىٰٓ أَنفُسِنَا ۖ وَغَرَّتْهُمُ ٱلْحَيَوٰةُ ٱلدُّنْيَا وَشَهِدُوا۟ عَلَىٰٓ أَنفُسِهِمْ أَنَّهُمْ كَانُوا۟ كَٰفِرِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ر س ل` — `B002`/`B005` (messenger and message / succession), `ق ص ص` — `B001`/`B002` (following a trace / sequential recounting), `ء ي ي` — `B002`/`B003` (directed mark / sign), and `ل ق ي` — `B004` (face-to-face meeting).** **Cue:** messengers sequentially traced signs toward the very meeting now occurring; `6:126` had detailed markers for rememberers, while `6:131` excludes destruction under heedlessness. **Role:** warning builds a continuous evidentiary path from prior sign to future encounter. **Abductive move:** I infer that present meeting verifies the route rather than introducing a new charge; report, signs, warning, and meeting are supplied, while route-verification is mine. **Reading change:** the interrogation reconstructs an unbroken warning chain whose endpoint the respondents now occupy.

2. **`ش ه د` — `B001`/`B002` (presence / testimony from knowledge), `ن ف س` — `B012`/`B013` (the very self / hidden intent), `غ ر ر` — `B008` (deceptive appearance), `د ن و` — `B002` (nearer side), and `ك ف ر` — `B001` (covering).** **Cue:** self-testimony appears twice around the explanation that near life deceived them; `6:128` likewise contained their own confession. **Role:** the self preserves knowledge beneath temporal covering, while near-life appearance suppresses that witness until full presence. **Abductive move:** I infer that deception displaced but did not erase inward testimony; repeated self-witness, covering, and final admission are supplied, while preservation-under-cover is mine. **Reading change:** judgment activates a witness the worldly system had kept latent inside the accused.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:131–132` confirmed the warning chain's judicial purpose: marked notice prevents wrongful destruction, and every subsequent work remains available as a graded trace.

## 6:131 — ذَٰلِكَ أَن لَّمْ يَكُن رَّبُّكَ مُهْلِكَ ٱلْقُرَىٰ بِظُلْمٍۢ وَأَهْلُهَا غَٰفِلُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ه ل ك` — `B001`/`B002` (ruin / self-casting into destruction), `ق ر ي` — `B001` (gathered settlement), `ظ ل م` — `B004` (acting in the wrong place or time), `ء ه ل` — `B001`/`B004` (people bound by belonging / familiar habitation), and `غ ف ل` — `B001`/`B006` (outside attention / unmarked).** **Cue:** the messenger audit in `6:130` supplies prior warning, and `6:132` affirms that no work escapes attention. **Role:** warning marks the familiar social field so consequence is not imposed where inhabitants had no locatable sign. **Abductive move:** I infer that informed self-direction is necessary for ruin to count as the community's own trajectory; warnings, self-witness, and nonheedless judgment are supplied, while necessity is mine. **Reading change:** the ayah defines justice as marked causal continuity between notice, communal conduct, and destruction.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:132` made non-heedless judgment granular, confirming that warned communal conduct is evaluated through every marked step rather than one undifferentiated collective label.

## 6:132 — وَلِكُلٍّۢ دَرَجَٰتٌۭ مِّمَّا عَمِلُوا۟ ۚ وَمَا رَبُّكَ بِغَٰفِلٍ عَمَّا يَعْمَلُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ك ل ل` — `B003` (totality), `د ر ج` — `B001`/`B003` (walking along a path / taking step by step), `ع م ل` — `B001`/`B011` (intentional work / traveled road), and `غ ف ل` — `B001`/`B002` (letting fall outside attention / leaving unmarked).** **Cue:** every person receives degrees from work after the warning-and-witness chain, while `6:133` shows whole generations can be replaced. **Role:** each work is a step that both advances a road and locates the agent at a corresponding degree; exhaustive attention prevents missing steps. **Abductive move:** I infer that rank is accumulated trajectory rather than a single terminal assignment; totality, degrees, work, and no heedlessness are supplied, while accumulation is mine. **Reading change:** the ayah makes judgment granular and process-based down to each traversed increment.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:133–134` qualified granular rank with generational contingency: every work locates a person, yet no present population is indispensable to the approaching promise.

## 6:133 — وَرَبُّكَ ٱلْغَنِىُّ ذُو ٱلرَّحْمَةِ ۚ إِن يَشَأْ يُذْهِبْكُمْ وَيَسْتَخْلِفْ مِنۢ بَعْدِكُم مَّا يَشَآءُ كَمَآ أَنشَأَكُم مِّن ذُرِّيَّةِ قَوْمٍ ءَاخَرِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`غ ن ي` — `B001`/`B002` (self-sufficiency / sufficing and replacing) and `ر ح م` — `B001` (mercy and tenderness).** **Cue:** self-sufficiency and mercy precede a replacement counterfactual; `6:132` made degrees depend on human work, while `6:134` guarantees the promise's arrival. **Role:** independence removes any need for the present community, while mercy explains continued nurture despite that dispensability. **Abductive move:** I infer that mercy is freely sustained precisely because no dependency coerces it; self-sufficiency, mercy, and possible removal are supplied, while freedom-from-need is mine. **Reading change:** the pair makes mercy nontransactional and creaturely privilege radically contingent.

2. **`ذ ه ب` — `B006` (going and departure), `خ ل ف` — `B001`/`B010` (one standing after another / renewal after loss), `ن ش ء` — `B006` (bringing into being), and `ذ ر ر` — `B001`/`B004` (scattered motes / spreading emergence).** **Cue:** current people can depart and successors stand after them just as they emerged from another people's descendants; `6:135` tells each side to work from its present position. **Role:** generations are replaceable occupancy waves assembled from dispersed prior seed. **Abductive move:** I infer that no community owns its historical station; departure, succession, origin from descendants, and present work are supplied, while station-tenancy is mine. **Reading change:** collective continuity becomes a relay of temporary occupants rather than guaranteed inheritance.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:134–135` made historical tenancy actionable: the future overtakes each temporary occupant while parallel work from present positions determines the visible aftermath.

## 6:134 — إِنَّ مَا تُوعَدُونَ لَءَاتٍۢ ۖ وَمَآ أَنتُم بِمُعْجِزِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`و ع د` — `B001`/`B002`/`B006` (promise opening expectation / threat / promising sign), `ء ت ي` — `B001`/`B011` (coming / overtaking by ruin), and `ع ج ز` — `B001`/`B002`/`B004` (falling short / eluding pursuit / rear).** **Cue:** the promised object is grammatically made an active comer, while the addressees cannot outstrip it; `6:135` directs them to work until the outcome becomes known. **Role:** promise opens a forward horizon, arrival pursues from it, and attempted escape cannot keep the event behind the runner. **Abductive move:** I infer a pursuit geometry in which the future closes distance regardless of present station; promise, coming, and nonelusion are supplied, while pursuit is mine. **Reading change:** fulfillment is not passively awaited but actively overtakes those moving through time.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:135` confirmed the pursuit geometry, because the approaching promise lets both courses mature until their rear traces disclose which position produced the house's outcome.

## 6:135 — قُلْ يَٰقَوْمِ ٱعْمَلُوا۟ عَلَىٰ مَكَانَتِكُمْ إِنِّى عَامِلٌۭ ۖ فَسَوْفَ تَعْلَمُونَ مَن تَكُونُ لَهُۥ عَٰقِبَةُ ٱلدَّارِ ۗ إِنَّهُۥ لَا يُفْلِحُ ٱلظَّٰلِمُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ك و ن` — `B002` (place, position, or rank), `ع م ل` — `B001`/`B011` (intentional work / traveled road), and `ع ق ب` — `B002`/`B006` (rear trace / outcome).** **Cue:** both community and messenger work from their positions, and future knowledge is assigned to the house's outcome; `6:134` has made that future an approaching event. **Role:** position is the starting coordinate, work lays the road, and the rear trace eventually discloses where each course led. **Abductive move:** I infer a controlled comparison between two trajectories permitted to mature under the same approaching horizon; parallel work and future outcome are supplied, while comparison model is mine. **Reading change:** the challenge asks history to expose each program through its produced aftermath.

2. **`د و ر` — `B003`/`B004` (circulation of states / adverse turn), `ف ل ح` — `B001`/`B005` (cleaving ground / lasting success), and `ظ ل م` — `B004` (misplacing action).** **Cue:** the house has a final turn, and wrongdoers are denied flourishing; `6:136–140` show a misallocated resource system destroying its own children and provision. **Role:** injustice misplaces productive cuts and shares, so the circulating household cannot yield lasting growth. **Abductive move:** I infer that failed flourishing is materially generated by the allocation practices about to be described; outcome, injustice, and following resource rules are supplied, while economic causality is mine. **Reading change:** the verdict anticipates systemic sterility, not only later punishment.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:136–140` supplied the promised systemic aftermath: misallocated shares, child-killing, and barred provision turn unjust work into a household that liquidates its own future.

## 6:136 — وَجَعَلُوا۟ لِلَّهِ مِمَّا ذَرَأَ مِنَ ٱلْحَرْثِ وَٱلْأَنْعَٰمِ نَصِيبًۭا فَقَالُوا۟ هَٰذَا لِلَّهِ بِزَعْمِهِمْ وَهَٰذَا لِشُرَكَآئِنَا ۖ فَمَا كَانَ لِشُرَكَآئِهِمْ فَلَا يَصِلُ إِلَى ٱللَّهِ ۖ وَمَا كَانَ لِلَّهِ فَهُوَ يَصِلُ إِلَىٰ شُرَكَآئِهِمْ ۗ سَآءَ مَا يَحْكُمُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ذ ر ء` — `B001`/`B003` (bringing creatures forth / sowing), `ح ر ث` — `B001`/`B002` (earning work / cultivation), `ن ع م` — `B001`/`B005` (bestowed well-being / grazing livestock), and `ن ص ب` — `B005` (assigned share).** **Cue:** humans assign portions from crops and animals that God brought forth; `6:141–142` will restore direct eating, due right, and provision. **Role:** the source produces the entire yield, cultivation develops it, and human apportionment overlays a derivative accounting grid. **Abductive move:** I infer that the scheme converts stewardship into ownership by treating source-gift as divisible private jurisdiction; origination, produce, and assigned shares are supplied, while jurisdictional conversion is mine. **Reading change:** the offense begins before unequal transfer, in assuming authority to partition the source's claim.

2. **`ز ع م` — `B001`/`B003` (uncertain claim / undertaking surety), `ش ر ك` — `B001` (partnership), `و ص ل` — `B001`/`B002`/`B006` (joining / reaching a destination / uninterrupted linking), and `ح ك م` — `B002` (adjudication).** **Cue:** claimed shares are tested by actual flow: partners' portion never reaches God, while God's portion leaks to partners. **Role:** transfer direction reveals which claimant the system operationally privileges, despite nominal balance. **Abductive move:** I infer that asymmetric leakage is a diagnostic of hidden governance; both allocation rules and bad judgment are supplied, while flow-audit is mine. **Reading change:** partnership is exposed as a one-way extraction system rather than genuine shared allocation.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:137–139` exposed the flow-audit's stakes: one-way partner extraction expands into control over offspring, access, animal use, naming, and gendered consumption.

## 6:137 — وَكَذَٰلِكَ زَيَّنَ لِكَثِيرٍۢ مِّنَ ٱلْمُشْرِكِينَ قَتْلَ أَوْلَٰدِهِمْ شُرَكَآؤُهُمْ لِيُرْدُوهُمْ وَلِيَلْبِسُوا۟ عَلَيْهِمْ دِينَهُمْ ۖ وَلَوْ شَآءَ ٱللَّهُ مَا فَعَلُوهُ ۖ فَذَرْهُمْ وَمَا يَفْتَرُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ز ي ن` — `B002` (making beauty show), `ق ت ل` — `B001`/`B005` (killing / exposing someone to killing), `و ل د` — `B001`/`B005` (offspring / derived generation), and `ر د ي` — `B003` (falling into ruin).** **Cue:** after resource shares flow toward partners, those partners make destruction of the next generation attractive; `6:140` names child-killing and provision-ban as loss. **Role:** adornment disguises a system consuming its own reproductive continuity, and ruin is the terminal demographic effect. **Abductive move:** I infer that partner institutions preserve their claims by extracting even from offspring, converting social reproduction into sacrifice; allocation, partners, child-killing, and ruin are supplied, while institutional extraction is mine. **Reading change:** the practice is systemic self-liquidation under an attractive religious surface.

2. **`ل ب س` — `B001`/`B003` (clothing / confusing mixture), `د ي ن` — `B001`/`B002` (obedience / recompense), `ف ع ل` — `B004` (contrived fabrication), and `ف ر ي` — `B001`/`B003` (cutting and stitching / inventing).** **Cue:** partners clothe religion while fabricated animal rules continue in `6:138–139`; divine willing is affirmed but the messenger is told to leave their inventions. **Role:** invented fragments are stitched into a religious garment that hides the economic and lethal mechanism beneath. **Abductive move:** I infer that ritual coherence legitimates practices that would otherwise appear self-destructive; clothing, religion, action, and fabrication are supplied, while legitimation is mine. **Reading change:** confusion is manufactured through a wearable system of rules, not accidental misunderstanding.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:138–140` made the religious garment fully inspectable as coordinated gatekeeping, incoherent classification, and eventual destruction of both heirs and provision.

## 6:138 — وَقَالُوا۟ هَٰذِهِۦٓ أَنْعَٰمٌۭ وَحَرْثٌ حِجْرٌۭ لَّا يَطْعَمُهَآ إِلَّا مَن نَّشَآءُ بِزَعْمِهِمْ وَأَنْعَٰمٌ حُرِّمَتْ ظُهُورُهَا وَأَنْعَٰمٌۭ لَّا يَذْكُرُونَ ٱسْمَ ٱللَّهِ عَلَيْهَا ٱفْتِرَآءً عَلَيْهِ ۚ سَيَجْزِيهِم بِمَا كَانُوا۟ يَفْتَرُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ح ج ر` — `B001`/`B004`/`B006` (keeping back by enclosure / enclosed place / circling boundary), `ط ع م` — `B001`/`B004` (taking food / provision), and `ش ي ء` — the `B001` will variant.** **Cue:** access to livestock and crops is enclosed for only those whom the rule-makers will, after the one-way share system of `6:136`; `6:141–142` will command broad eating from divine provision. **Role:** a manufactured boundary converts common created provision into controlled access. **Abductive move:** I infer that claimed sanctity functions as gatekeeping power over food; enclosure, selected eaters, and prior partner allocation are supplied, while gatekeeping is mine. **Reading change:** `حِجْرٌ` becomes an economic perimeter disguised as sacred restriction.

2. **`ح ر م` — `B003`/`B006` (protected precinct / inviolable claim), `ظ ه ر` — `B002`/`B006` (back / support), `ذ ك ر` — `B004` (mention), `س م و` — `B005` (name as designation), and `ف ر ي` — `B003` (fabricating).** **Cue:** one animal class has its supporting back barred, another lacks divine naming, and all rules are called fabrication; `6:139` adds gendered internal contents. **Role:** invented sanctity separately controls food, transport/support, and ritual provenance. **Abductive move:** I infer a total access regime assembled by segmenting animal functions; each restriction is supplied, while regime integration is mine. **Reading change:** the verse catalogs coordinated control over use, mobility, and naming rather than unrelated taboos.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:139–140` confirmed that the access regime governs hidden contents and recipients, then named its combined demographic and nutritional result as loss.

## 6:139 — وَقَالُوا۟ مَا فِى بُطُونِ هَٰذِهِ ٱلْأَنْعَٰمِ خَالِصَةٌۭ لِّذُكُورِنَا وَمُحَرَّمٌ عَلَىٰٓ أَزْوَٰجِنَا ۖ وَإِن يَكُن مَّيْتَةًۭ فَهُمْ فِيهِ شُرَكَآءُ ۚ سَيَجْزِيهِمْ وَصْفَهُمْ ۚ إِنَّهُۥ حَكِيمٌ عَلِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **`ب ط ن` — `B001`/`B002` (belly / hidden inward reality), `خ ل ص` — `B001`/`B004` (unmixed purity / exclusive separation), `ذ ك ر` — `B001` (male), and `ز و ج` — `B001`/`B002` (paired counterpart / spouse).** **Cue:** hidden animal contents are declared exclusively male and barred to paired women; if dead, the same parties share them. **Role:** exclusive purity is assigned by gender while material state-change abruptly cancels it. **Abductive move:** I infer that the rule protects social privilege rather than a stable property of the contents, because death reverses allocation without changing the recipients' sexes; all conditions are supplied, while privilege diagnosis is mine. **Reading change:** the internal substance becomes an audit revealing the rule's incoherent social purpose.

2. **`م و ت` — `B012` (lifeless state), `ش ر ك` — `B001` (sharing), `و ص ف` — `B001`/`B002` (showing by description / selling by unseen description), `ج ز ي` — `B001` (matching recompense), and `ح ك م` — `B003` (wise knowledge).** **Cue:** death toggles prohibition into sharing, and their very descriptions become the object of recompense. **Role:** description manufactures legal reality around unseen contents, while wisdom knows the material and social truth the description obscures. **Abductive move:** I infer that recompensing “their description” targets rule-making speech as causal action; description, allocation change, and recompense are supplied, while speech-as-governance is mine. **Reading change:** the verse treats classification itself as accountable conduct.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:140–141` made classificatory speech visibly causal: one description depletes the whole system, while the corrective rule restores diverse growth, direct use, due shares, and limits.

## 6:140 — قَدْ خَسِرَ ٱلَّذِينَ قَتَلُوٓا۟ أَوْلَٰدَهُمْ سَفَهًۢا بِغَيْرِ عِلْمٍۢ وَحَرَّمُوا۟ مَا رَزَقَهُمُ ٱللَّهُ ٱفْتِرَآءً عَلَى ٱللَّهِ ۚ قَدْ ضَلُّوا۟ وَمَا كَانُوا۟ مُهْتَدِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`خ س ر` — `B001`/`B002` (deficiency / trade loss), `ق ت ل` — `B001` (killing), `و ل د` — `B001`/`B005` (offspring / generated continuation), `ح ر م` — `B001`/`B008` (barring / deprivation), and `ر ز ق` — `B001`/`B002` (allotted gift / nourishment).** **Cue:** child-killing and banning provision are joined as one loss after the partner allocation system; `6:141` immediately restores fruit, eating, due right, and limits. **Role:** the scheme destroys both future recipients and present nourishment, liquidating reproduction and sustenance together. **Abductive move:** I infer that this is a total balance-sheet loss generated by invented piety; children, provision, killing, forbidding, and loss are supplied, while balance-sheet integration is mine. **Reading change:** `خَسِرَ` names systemic self-depletion, not only moral condemnation.

2. **`س ف ه` — `B001`/`B002`/`B003` (unstable lightness / lightness of mind / turning aside by deception), `ف ر ي` — `B003` (fabricating), `ض ل ل` — `B001` (straying), and `ه د ي` — `B002` (intended course).** **Cue:** unknowing instability drives the two destructive rules, which are falsely attached to God; the next ayah supplies a measured production-distribution course. **Role:** folly destabilizes valuation until gifts can be recoded as forbidden and heirs as expendable. **Abductive move:** I infer that fabricated authority provides apparent weight to otherwise unstable judgment; folly, invention, and misdirection are supplied, while borrowed-weight mechanism is mine. **Reading change:** the practices persist because false divine attribution anchors a light, wavering mind.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:141–142` supplied the stable alternative to borrowed authority: source-created diversity is distributed by timed right and material function rather than fabricated prohibition.

## 6:141 — ۞ وَهُوَ ٱلَّذِىٓ أَنشَأَ جَنَّٰتٍۢ مَّعْرُوشَٰتٍۢ وَغَيْرَ مَعْرُوشَٰتٍۢ وَٱلنَّخْلَ وَٱلزَّرْعَ مُخْتَلِفًا أُكُلُهُۥ وَٱلزَّيْتُونَ وَٱلرُّمَّانَ مُتَشَٰبِهًۭا وَغَيْرَ مُتَشَٰبِهٍۢ ۚ كُلُوا۟ مِن ثَمَرِهِۦٓ إِذَآ أَثْمَرَ وَءَاتُوا۟ حَقَّهُۥ يَوْمَ حَصَادِهِۦ ۖ وَلَا تُسْرِفُوٓا۟ ۚ إِنَّهُۥ لَا يُحِبُّ ٱلْمُسْرِفِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ن ش ء` — `B006` (bringing into being), `ع ر ش` — `B002`/`B003` (raised support / trellising), `ز ر ع` — `B001`/`B003` (cultivation / agricultural dealing), `خ ل ف` — `B004` (taking different ways), and `ش ب ه` — `B001`/`B002` (resemblance / ambiguity).** **Cue:** created production includes supported and unsupported gardens, varied yields, and similar/dissimilar fruits, countering the rigid fabricated classes of `6:138–140`. **Role:** legitimate order accommodates structural and visible diversity without turning difference into arbitrary prohibition. **Abductive move:** I infer that variation is an intended production feature, while the prior scheme converted variation into access hierarchy; created diversity and fabricated rules are supplied, while contrast is mine. **Reading change:** the ayah replaces classificatory control with observational stewardship of diverse growth.

2. **`ء ك ل` — `B002`/`B003` (yield / consumable provision), `ث م ر` — `B001`/`B003` (ripe fruit / benefit), `ح ق ق` — `B002` (due claim), `ح ص د` — `B001`/`B003` (harvest cutting / tightly made), and `س ر ف` — `B001` (overstepping the limit).** **Cue:** eating is permitted, a due is paid at the exact harvest moment, and excess is barred; `6:142` repeats eating from provision and rejects another path. **Role:** harvest converts growth into divisible yield, at which point another's due attaches before consumption crosses into excess. **Abductive move:** I infer a timed distribution architecture balancing use, obligation, and ecological/social limit; fruit, harvest, right, eating, and no excess are supplied, while architecture is mine. **Reading change:** the counter-rule is neither unrestricted consumption nor arbitrary taboo but calibrated flow from source to eater and claimant.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:142–144` confirmed observational stewardship, because useful animal distinctions remain while exhaustive pair enumeration dismantles arbitrary sacred exclusions.

## 6:142 — وَمِنَ ٱلْأَنْعَٰمِ حَمُولَةًۭ وَفَرْشًۭا ۚ كُلُوا۟ مِمَّا رَزَقَكُمُ ٱللَّهُ وَلَا تَتَّبِعُوا۟ خُطُوَٰتِ ٱلشَّيْطَٰنِ ۚ إِنَّهُۥ لَكُمْ عَدُوٌّۭ مُّبِينٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **`ح م ل` — `B001`/`B006` (bearing a load / carrying mount), `ف ر ش` — `B001`/`B004` (spread low / small livestock), `ر ز ق` — `B001` (allotted gift), and `ء ك ل` — `B003` (consumable provision).** **Cue:** livestock diversity is organized by useful scale and function, then direct eating from divine provision is allowed; earlier fabricated rules barred backs and selected eaters. **Role:** load animals and low livestock form complementary provisioning functions without invented sacred ownership. **Abductive move:** I infer that functional distinction is legitimate when it serves creaturely use rather than partner extraction; animal classes, provision, and eating are supplied, while function-versus-control contrast is mine. **Reading change:** categorization is retained but returned to material utility.

2. **`ت ب ع` — `B003` (stepwise tracking), `خ ط و` — `B001`/`B002` (stride-space / followed traces), `ش ط ن` — `B001` (remoteness), and `ع د و` — `B003`/`B008` (enemy / successive pursuit).** **Cue:** direct provision is contrasted with following an enemy's steps; `6:143–144` will dismantle prohibitions through exhaustive pairs. **Role:** each small step leaves a trace that turns simple gift into increasingly remote rule, while enmity pursues through succession rather than one leap. **Abductive move:** I infer that the fabricated system accumulated incrementally from minor classifications into lethal practice; step-following and the preceding rule sequence are supplied, while incremental escalation is mine. **Reading change:** the warning identifies path dependence at the smallest unit of imitation.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:143–144` identified the enemy's smallest followed steps as unsupported exceptions, then removed them through a complete category grid and provenance audit.

## 6:143 — ثَمَٰنِيَةَ أَزْوَٰجٍۢ ۖ مِّنَ ٱلضَّأْنِ ٱثْنَيْنِ وَمِنَ ٱلْمَعْزِ ٱثْنَيْنِ ۗ قُلْ ءَآلذَّكَرَيْنِ حَرَّمَ أَمِ ٱلْأُنثَيَيْنِ أَمَّا ٱشْتَمَلَتْ عَلَيْهِ أَرْحَامُ ٱلْأُنثَيَيْنِ ۖ نَبِّـُٔونِى بِعِلْمٍ إِن كُنتُمْ صَٰدِقِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ث م ن` — `B002`/`B001` (eight / price in exchange), `ز و ج` — `B001`/`B004` (paired counterpart / kind), and `ث ن ي` — `B001`/`B008` (making two / excepting from a whole).** **Cue:** the inventory counts sheep and goats by pairs before asking which possible class was excepted as forbidden; `6:144` repeats the same audit for camels and cattle. **Role:** complete enumeration prices every claimed exception against the whole categorical space. **Abductive move:** I infer that an unsupported ban must identify a stable subset across the exhaustive pair grid; number, pairs, alternatives, and demand for knowledge are supplied, while grid-audit is mine. **Reading change:** the questions become a formal test showing that the prohibition cannot be consistently located.

2. **`ذ ك ر` — `B001` (male), `ء ن ث` — `B001` (female), `ش م ل` — `B001`/`B005` (all-around encompassing / concealed carried thing), and `ر ح م` — `B003` (womb).** **Cue:** male, female, and whatever female wombs encompass exhaust sex and gestational content; the prior rules had assigned hidden contents by recipient gender. **Role:** the womb contains the very categories the invented rule tries to separate from outside. **Abductive move:** I infer that common gestational enclosure undermines an absolute purity hierarchy between outputs; categories and encompassing womb are supplied, while hierarchy critique is mine. **Reading change:** reproductive anatomy becomes evidence against selective sacred exclusion.

3. **`ن ب ء` — `B002` (reported news), `ع ل م` — `B001`/`B002` (clear knowledge / distinguishing mark), and `ص د ق` — `B001`/`B004` (truth / fulfillment).** **Cue:** after exhaustive questioning, claimants are told to produce an informed report if truthful; `6:144` escalates to eyewitness or divine charge. **Role:** a valid prohibition needs reportable evidence that marks which class and fulfills the claim's burden. **Abductive move:** I infer an evidentiary burden shifted onto the rule-makers; demand, knowledge, and truth condition are supplied, while burden-shift is mine. **Reading change:** inherited taboo is treated as a falsifiable claim, not self-authenticating custom.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:144–145` escalated the falsifiable-claim test from informed report to witnessed commission and finally to the actual source-bounded inventory found in revelation.

## 6:144 — وَمِنَ ٱلْإِبِلِ ٱثْنَيْنِ وَمِنَ ٱلْبَقَرِ ٱثْنَيْنِ ۗ قُلْ ءَآلذَّكَرَيْنِ حَرَّمَ أَمِ ٱلْأُنثَيَيْنِ أَمَّا ٱشْتَمَلَتْ عَلَيْهِ أَرْحَامُ ٱلْأُنثَيَيْنِ ۖ أَمْ كُنتُمْ شُهَدَآءَ إِذْ وَصَّىٰكُمُ ٱللَّهُ بِهَٰذَا ۚ فَمَنْ أَظْلَمُ مِمَّنِ ٱفْتَرَىٰ عَلَى ٱللَّهِ كَذِبًۭا لِّيُضِلَّ ٱلنَّاسَ بِغَيْرِ عِلْمٍ ۗ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ لَا يَهْدِى ٱلْقَوْمَ ٱلظَّٰلِمِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ء ب ل` — `B001` (camels and their tending), `ب ق ر` — `B001`/`B002` (cattle / splitting open), `ث ن ي` — `B001`/`B008` (pairing / excepting), `ذ ك ر` — `B001` (male), `ء ن ث` — `B001` (female), and `ش م ل` — `B001` (all-around encompassing).** **Cue:** the pair-grid from `6:143` is repeated for the remaining livestock kinds and again exhausts male, female, and gestational contents. **Role:** repeated enumeration closes every categorical opening through which an arbitrary exception might be inserted. **Abductive move:** I infer that the two ayat form one exhaustive matrix across four species and reproductive states; lists and repeated questions are supplied, while matrix completeness is mine. **Reading change:** the audit demonstrates by coverage that the inherited ban has no stable object.

2. **`ش ه د` — `B001`/`B002` (presence with witnessing / testimony from knowledge) and `و ص ي` — `B001`/`B002` (joining one thing to another / entrusted charge).** **Cue:** after asking for informed report, this ayah asks whether claimants were present when God joined the alleged rule to them as a charge; `6:145` supplies what is actually found in revelation. **Role:** eyewitness and transmitted commission are two possible provenance links, both absent. **Abductive move:** I infer an explicit source-authentication test: a divine rule needs either witnessed issuance or reliable joined transmission; witness, charge, and fabrication contrast are supplied, while two-route authentication is mine. **Reading change:** customary certainty is dismantled by requiring a traceable chain of authority.

3. **`ف ر ي` — `B001`/`B003` (cutting and stitching / fabricating), `ك ذ ب` — `B004` (failing a charge), `ض ل ل` — `B001` (misguiding from an aim), `ع ل م` — `B001` (knowledge), and `ظ ل م` — `B004` (misplacing).** **Cue:** unsupported prohibition is attached to God and used to redirect people; the close denies guidance to the unjust group. **Role:** fabrication supplies a counterfeit transmission link whose social function is route control. **Abductive move:** I infer that false provenance is more culpable because it governs others' movement, not merely the speaker's diet; fabrication, people, and misguidance are supplied, while governance amplification is mine. **Reading change:** the lie is a public navigation offense.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:145` supplied the authentic joined transmission that the alleged charge lacked, confirming false provenance as a public navigation offense rather than private custom.

## 6:145 — قُل لَّآ أَجِدُ فِى مَآ أُوحِىَ إِلَىَّ مُحَرَّمًا عَلَىٰ طَاعِمٍۢ يَطْعَمُهُۥٓ إِلَّآ أَن يَكُونَ مَيْتَةً أَوْ دَمًۭا مَّسْفُوحًا أَوْ لَحْمَ خِنزِيرٍۢ فَإِنَّهُۥ رِجْسٌ أَوْ فِسْقًا أُهِلَّ لِغَيْرِ ٱللَّهِ بِهِۦ ۚ فَمَنِ ٱضْطُرَّ غَيْرَ بَاغٍۢ وَلَا عَادٍۢ فَإِنَّ رَبَّكَ غَفُورٌۭ رَّحِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **`و ج د` — `B001`/`B002` (finding / being found in reality), `و ح ي` — `B001`/`B004` (hidden imparting / divine message), `ح ر م` — `B001`/`B003` (barring / protected bound), and `ط ع م` — `B001` (taking food).** **Cue:** after inherited bans fail source-authentication, the messenger reports exactly what is found in revelation; `6:146` distinguishes a historically specific additional restriction. **Role:** textual finding, not social claim, locates the protected food boundary. **Abductive move:** I infer that the list is intentionally closed by evidence available in the message, not by speculative extension; finding, revelation, prohibition, and enumerated exceptions are supplied, while closure principle is mine. **Reading change:** lawful restriction becomes a source-bounded inventory.

2. **`د م و` — `B001` (blood), `س ف ح` — `B001` (pouring liquid), `ل ح م` — `B001`/`B005` (flesh / adhering), `ر ج س` — `B001` (taint), `ف س ق` — `B002` (emerging from a peel), and `ه ل ل` — `B004` (raising the voice in proclamation).** **Cue:** the prohibited items are materially or relationally specified: dead state, poured blood, swine flesh, taint, and voiced dedication to another. **Role:** the rule distinguishes bodily substance from provenance-marking proclamation; *fisq* names exit from the protective source relation. **Abductive move:** I infer that impurity here spans both material condition and allegiance encoded at slaughter; list and proclamation are supplied, while two-domain integration is mine. **Reading change:** the food boundary is neither purely hygienic nor purely verbal but a joined material-ritual classification.

3. **`ض ر ر` — `B003` (compulsion under necessity), `ب غ ي` — `B001`/`B003` (seeking / overstepping), `ع د و` — `B001` (crossing a bound), `غ ف ر` — `B001`/`B002` (protective covering / shielding consequence), and `ر ح م` — `B001` (mercy).** **Cue:** necessity permits intake only where the eater neither seeks the forbidden state nor crosses its limit; `6:147` expands mercy while preserving force against criminals. **Role:** the exception protects life under pressure, and the two qualifications keep emergency passage from becoming a new route of desire. **Abductive move:** I infer a narrow corridor through the boundary whose width is set by need; compulsion, nonseeking, nontransgression, and forgiveness are supplied, while corridor geometry is mine. **Reading change:** mercy operates as calibrated boundary permeability.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:146–147` refined source-bounded restriction through a historically indexed rule and expansive mercy, preventing either speculative widening or mechanical rigidity.

## 6:146 — وَعَلَى ٱلَّذِينَ هَادُوا۟ حَرَّمْنَا كُلَّ ذِى ظُفُرٍۢ ۖ وَمِنَ ٱلْبَقَرِ وَٱلْغَنَمِ حَرَّمْنَا عَلَيْهِمْ شُحُومَهُمَآ إِلَّا مَا حَمَلَتْ ظُهُورُهُمَآ أَوِ ٱلْحَوَايَآ أَوْ مَا ٱخْتَلَطَ بِعَظْمٍۢ ۚ ذَٰلِكَ جَزَيْنَٰهُم بِبَغْيِهِمْ ۖ وَإِنَّا لَصَٰدِقُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ظ ف ر` — `B002` (nail, claw, or talon), `ش ح م` — `B001`/`B006` (fat / inner fat-like part), `ح م ل` — `B001` (bearing), `ظ ه ر` — `B002` (back), `ح و ي` — `B001`/`B003` (holding / inward gut coils), `خ ل ط` — `B001` (material mixing), and `ع ظ م` — `B005` (bone).** **Cue:** the restriction is anatomically mapped, then exceptions are identified by where fat is borne, enclosed, or mixed; `6:145` had supplied a concise general inventory. **Role:** body structure, not invented gender or status categories, articulates the exceptions. **Abductive move:** I infer that the specificity prevents arbitrary expansion by tying each allowance to inspectable anatomy; claws, fat, back, guts, and bone are supplied, while anti-expansion function is mine. **Reading change:** the rule is a materially jointed map rather than an open-ended taboo.

2. **`ج ز ي` — `B001` (recompense matching an act), `ب غ ي` — `B003` (overstepping into oppression), `ص د ق` — `B001`/`B004` (truth / fulfillment), and `ه و د` — `B002` (Jewish community).** **Cue:** the additional restriction is assigned to a particular community and explicitly described as recompense for transgression. **Role:** historical causation bounds the rule to its recipient and explains its difference from the preceding general list. **Abductive move:** I infer that removing this causal qualifier would misapply a responsive penalty as a universal ontology of food; community, recompense, and transgression are supplied, while anti-universalization is mine. **Reading change:** restriction becomes historically indexed moral response, not evidence for the fabricated bans under audit.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:147–148` confirmed historical indexing: a responsive restriction cannot validate invented universal taboos, and sovereignty cannot erase responsibility for making them.

## 6:147 — فَإِن كَذَّبُوكَ فَقُل رَّبُّكُمْ ذُو رَحْمَةٍۢ وَٰسِعَةٍۢ وَلَا يُرَدُّ بَأْسُهُۥ عَنِ ٱلْقَوْمِ ٱلْمُجْرِمِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ر ح م` — `B001`/`B003` (mercy / womb), `و س ع` — `B001`/`B002` (spaciousness / capacity to contain), `ر د د` — `B002` (repelling), `ب ء س` — `B001` (severe force), and `ج ر م` — `B003`/`B004` (incurring / guilt).** **Cue:** expansive mercy answers denial, but severe force cannot be repelled from those who have incurred guilt; `6:145` already showed mercy opening a necessity corridor, and `6:148` tests agency claims. **Role:** mercy provides a wide containing field for return, while acquired criminality can mature into an event that no later deflection repels. **Abductive move:** I infer temporal layering: breadth operates before closure, not as cancellation of every consequence; mercy, denial, incurment, and unrepelled force are supplied, while temporal layering is mine. **Reading change:** mercy and severity become successive possibilities within one capacious governance rather than contradictory attributes.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:148–149` showed why mercy's breadth is temporal rather than exculpatory: unsupported excuses remain answerable to a proof that reaches its evidentiary limit.

## 6:148 — سَيَقُولُ ٱلَّذِينَ أَشْرَكُوا۟ لَوْ شَآءَ ٱللَّهُ مَآ أَشْرَكْنَا وَلَآ ءَابَآؤُنَا وَلَا حَرَّمْنَا مِن شَىْءٍۢ ۚ كَذَٰلِكَ كَذَّبَ ٱلَّذِينَ مِن قَبْلِهِمْ حَتَّىٰ ذَاقُوا۟ بَأْسَنَا ۗ قُلْ هَلْ عِندَكُم مِّنْ عِلْمٍۢ فَتُخْرِجُوهُ لَنَآ ۖ إِن تَتَّبِعُونَ إِلَّا ٱلظَّنَّ وَإِنْ أَنتُمْ إِلَّا تَخْرُصُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ش ي ء` — the `B001` will variant, `ش ر ك` — `B001` (partnership), `ء ب و` — `B001` (parental formation), and `ح ر م` — `B001` (forbidding).** **Cue:** divine will is invoked to erase responsibility for both current and ancestral partnership and prohibition; `6:149` affirms that the same will could have guided all, yet calls the argument conclusive. **Role:** the speakers collapse permission, inherited formation, and accountable action into one causal level. **Abductive move:** I infer that the appeal uses sovereignty to make every enacted distinction evidentially meaningless; will, their acts, ancestors, and following proof are supplied, while level-collapse diagnosis is mine. **Reading change:** the excuse is exposed as a category error about causal permission, not a denial of divine will.

2. **`ذ و ق` — `B002` (experiencing and testing), `ب ء س` — `B001` (severe force), `ع ل م` — `B001` (knowledge), and `خ ر ج` — `B002` (bringing out).** **Cue:** prior deniers maintained the same claim until consequence was tasted; current speakers are challenged to bring out actual knowledge. **Role:** tasting supplies experiential falsification, while producing knowledge is the unmet evidentiary burden. **Abductive move:** I infer that consequence tests the practical truth of claimed nonresponsibility when no theoretical evidence can be produced; historical tasting and present challenge are supplied, while falsification relation is mine. **Reading change:** the excuse is bracketed by failed output: no knowledge comes out now, and severe experience comes in later.

3. **`ت ب ع` — `B003` (stepwise tracking), `ظ ن ن` — `B003` (uncertain supposition), and `خ ر ص` — `B001`/`B002` (conjectural measure / speech without knowledge).** **Cue:** the same conjecture chain condemned in `6:116` reappears after a long material and source audit. **Role:** supposition is measured into a claim and then followed as inherited practice. **Abductive move:** I infer that repetition here retrospectively classifies all intervening invented rules as outputs of the same unsupported estimation process; repeated roots and rule sequence are supplied, while retrospective classification is mine. **Reading change:** the ayah reveals a stable epistemic engine beneath diverse taboos.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:149–151` dismantled causal level-collapse: universal guidance is a distinct counterfactual, while completed proof, failed witnesses, and actual prohibitions preserve accountable response.

## 6:149 — قُلْ فَلِلَّهِ ٱلْحُجَّةُ ٱلْبَٰلِغَةُ ۖ فَلَوْ شَآءَ لَهَدَىٰكُمْ أَجْمَعِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ح ج ج` — `B002`/`B003` (clear intended road / prevailing proof), `ب ل غ` — `B001`/`B004` (reaching the limit / expression reaching its intent), and `ج م ع` — `B001`/`B009` (gathering all / wholeness without loss).** **Cue:** the proof follows exhaustive category questions, source demands, a revealed list, historical qualification, and refutation of conjecture; universal guidance remains a distinct counterfactual of will. **Role:** the argument reaches every relevant evidentiary endpoint without needing to coerce every recipient into one response. **Abductive move:** I infer that proof-completeness and response-uniformity are deliberately separated; reaching proof, possible gathered guidance, and prior divergent response are supplied, while separation is mine. **Reading change:** `ٱلْحُجَّةُ ٱلْبَٰلِغَةُ` is conclusive because it completes the route of evidence, not because it mechanically produces assent.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:150–151` confirmed that conclusive proof need not compel assent, because it can expose contaminated testimony and replace it with a traceable recitation of the governing rules.

## 6:150 — قُلْ هَلُمَّ شُهَدَآءَكُمُ ٱلَّذِينَ يَشْهَدُونَ أَنَّ ٱللَّهَ حَرَّمَ هَٰذَا ۖ فَإِن شَهِدُوا۟ فَلَا تَشْهَدْ مَعَهُمْ ۚ وَلَا تَتَّبِعْ أَهْوَآءَ ٱلَّذِينَ كَذَّبُوا۟ بِـَٔايَٰتِنَا وَٱلَّذِينَ لَا يُؤْمِنُونَ بِٱلْءَاخِرَةِ وَهُم بِرَبِّهِمْ يَعْدِلُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ل م م` — `B001`/`B002` (gathering scattered things / bringing near), `ش ه د` — `B001`/`B002` (presence / informed testimony), and `ح ر م` — `B001` (forbidding).** **Cue:** claimants must assemble and bring their witnesses near after failing the knowledge challenge; even produced testimony is not to recruit the messenger. **Role:** gathering makes the alleged evidence inspectable, but presence alone does not establish informed truth. **Abductive move:** I infer a two-stage test of availability and independence: can witnesses appear, and can their testimony survive without interest alignment? The summons, testimony, and refusal to join are supplied, while two-stage testing is mine. **Reading change:** social attestation is admitted into evidence but denied automatic authority.

2. **`ت ب ع` — `B003` (stepwise tracking), `ه و ي` — `B002`/`B004` (falling / desire), `ك ذ ب` — `B002` (declaring signs false), and `ع د ل` — `B002`/`B006` (equivalence / deviation).** **Cue:** false witnesses belong to a desire-driven route shared by sign-deniers and those who make equivalents to their Lord; `6:151` replaces their testimony with sequential recitation of actual prohibitions. **Role:** desire tilts the evidentiary path, denial removes correcting markers, and false equivalence fixes the deviation into theology. **Abductive move:** I infer that testimony is disqualified by the process generating it, not merely by its conclusion; desires, denial, and equivalencing are supplied, while process-based disqualification is mine. **Reading change:** the messenger's nonparticipation protects the evidence chain from contaminated alignment.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:151–152` completed the process-based disqualification: contaminated testimony is replaced by coherent recitation, calibrated justice, and protected claims whose source and structure can be inspected.

## 6:151 — ۞ قُلْ تَعَالَوْا۟ أَتْلُ مَا حَرَّمَ رَبُّكُمْ عَلَيْكُمْ ۖ أَلَّا تُشْرِكُوا۟ بِهِۦ شَيْـًۭٔا ۖ وَبِٱلْوَٰلِدَيْنِ إِحْسَٰنًۭا ۖ وَلَا تَقْتُلُوٓا۟ أَوْلَٰدَكُم مِّنْ إِمْلَٰقٍۢ ۖ نَّحْنُ نَرْزُقُكُمْ وَإِيَّاهُمْ ۖ وَلَا تَقْرَبُوا۟ ٱلْفَوَٰحِشَ مَا ظَهَرَ مِنْهَا وَمَا بَطَنَ ۖ وَلَا تَقْتُلُوا۟ ٱلنَّفْسَ ٱلَّتِى حَرَّمَ ٱللَّهُ إِلَّا بِٱلْحَقِّ ۚ ذَٰلِكُمْ وَصَّىٰكُم بِهِۦ لَعَلَّكُمْ تَعْقِلُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ع ل و` — `B006`/`B001` (call to come up / rising), `ت ل و` — `B001`/`B004` (following in sequence / liability following its holder), `ح ر م` — `B003`/`B006` (protected precinct / inviolable claim), and `و ص ي` — `B001`/`B002` (joining / entrusted charge).** **Cue:** witnesses were summoned in `6:150`; now hearers are called upward to a sequential recitation of actual protected claims, ending in a joined charge. **Role:** recitation links each obligation into a followable chain whose liabilities stay attached to their holders. **Abductive move:** I infer that genuine prohibition is recognizable by coherent protection across relations, unlike isolated food taboos; sequence, protected life/kin, and charge are supplied, while coherence criterion is mine. **Reading change:** the list is a connected architecture of inviolability, not a miscellaneous code.

2. **`و ل د` — `B001`/`B002` (offspring / parents), `ح س ن` — `B002` (beneficent good action), `ق ت ل` — `B001` (killing), `م ل ق` — `B002` (stripped of means), and `ر ز ق` — `B001`/`B002` (allotted provision / nourishment).** **Cue:** beneficence runs upward to parents and protection downward to children; poverty is answered by first-person provision for both generations. **Role:** the generational chain is protected against economic panic by relocating sustenance to the source beyond the household's current means. **Abductive move:** I infer that fear of scarcity falsely makes offspring competitors for a fixed family store; poverty, killing, and divine provision for both are supplied, while fixed-store assumption is mine. **Reading change:** the prohibition repairs intergenerational care by correcting its resource model.

3. **`ق ر ب` — `B001`/`B007` (nearness / approaching), `ف ح ش` — `B001`/`B002` (gross ugliness / exceeding bounds), `ظ ه ر` — `B001` (outer manifestation), `ب ط ن` — `B002` (hidden interior), `ن ف س` — `B011` (living self), `ح ق ق` — `B002` (due right), and `ع ق ل` — `B001`/`B002` (understanding as restraint / tether).** **Cue:** the commands regulate approach before action, cover outer and inner obscenity, and protect life except by right; reason is the hoped result. **Role:** restraint operates as distance control around vulnerable boundaries, including invisible approach and irreversible killing. **Abductive move:** I infer that reason is practical tethering before threshold crossing, not post hoc explanation; approach, bounds, life, and restraint are supplied, while prevention model is mine. **Reading change:** `تَعْقِلُونَ` names the capacity to hold oneself outside destructive proximity.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:152–153` showed that the protections are one maintained route: fiduciary limits, fair measure, just speech, and covenant memory converge in a singular straight path.

## 6:152 — وَلَا تَقْرَبُوا۟ مَالَ ٱلْيَتِيمِ إِلَّا بِٱلَّتِى هِىَ أَحْسَنُ حَتَّىٰ يَبْلُغَ أَشُدَّهُۥ ۖ وَأَوْفُوا۟ ٱلْكَيْلَ وَٱلْمِيزَانَ بِٱلْقِسْطِ ۖ لَا نُكَلِّفُ نَفْسًا إِلَّا وُسْعَهَا ۖ وَإِذَا قُلْتُمْ فَٱعْدِلُوا۟ وَلَوْ كَانَ ذَا قُرْبَىٰ ۖ وَبِعَهْدِ ٱللَّهِ أَوْفُوا۟ ۚ ذَٰلِكُمْ وَصَّىٰكُم بِهِۦ لَعَلَّكُمْ تَذَكَّرُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ي ت م` — `B001`/`B004` (offspring cut off from a protecting parent / delayed care), `م و ل` — `B001` (wealth), `ق ر ب` — `B001`/`B007` (nearness / engaging), `ح س ن` — `B002` (skillful beneficence), `ب ل غ` — `B001` (reaching the limit), and `ش د د` — `B004` (full maturity and strength).** **Cue:** approach to orphan property is permitted only through the best care until the ward reaches strength; the prior ayah protected children under provision. **Role:** guardianship temporarily permits controlled nearness because parental protection is absent, but maturity sets the exit threshold. **Abductive move:** I infer a fiduciary bridge that must terminate when capacity arrives; orphanhood, property, best approach, and maturity are supplied, while bridge model is mine. **Reading change:** the rule defines stewardship by both purpose and expiration.

2. **`و ف ي` — `B001` (complete fullness), `ك ي ل` — `B001`/`B005` (measuring food / comparing options), `و ز ن` — `B001`/`B002`/`B003` (weight / justice-scale / balancing), `ق س ط` — `B001`/`B004` (equity / straight balance), `ك ل ف` — `B003` (undertaking a burden), and `و س ع` — `B002` (capacity to bear).** **Cue:** full commercial measure is followed by a capacity ceiling on obligation, then just speech and covenant completion. **Role:** the same calibration principle prevents both shorting another and overloading a self. **Abductive move:** I infer that justice is bidirectional proportionality: full due outward, no impossible burden inward; measure, capacity, and equity are supplied, while bidirectionality is mine. **Reading change:** the verse unifies market precision and merciful obligation under one scale.

3. **`ق و ل` — `B001` (uttered speech), `ع د ل` — `B001`/`B005` (justice / straightening), `ق ر ب` — `B003` (kinship), `ع ه د` — `B001`/`B002`/`B003` (tending over time / entrusted charge / covenant), and `ذ ك ر` — `B003`/`B009` (bringing to mind / reminder).** **Cue:** kinship is explicitly prevented from bending speech, and divine covenant must be fulfilled; remembrance closes the charge. **Role:** justice straightens testimony against relational pull, while covenant and memory preserve that line over time. **Abductive move:** I infer that repeated remembrance supplies the maintenance needed when kin pressure recurs; speech, kin, covenant, and memory are supplied, while maintenance is mine. **Reading change:** fairness is a long-term kept alignment, not a one-time neutral utterance.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:153–155` made bidirectional justice part of a larger transmissible architecture, because the singular route is completed, detailed, sent down, and followed under mercy.

## 6:153 — وَأَنَّ هَٰذَا صِرَٰطِى مُسْتَقِيمًۭا فَٱتَّبِعُوهُ ۖ وَلَا تَتَّبِعُوا۟ ٱلسُّبُلَ فَتَفَرَّقَ بِكُمْ عَن سَبِيلِهِۦ ۚ ذَٰلِكُمْ وَصَّىٰكُم بِهِۦ لَعَلَّكُمْ تَتَّقُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ص ر ط` — `B001`/`B002` (straight road / passage that swallows movement), `ق و م` — `B008` (straightness), `ت ب ع` — `B003`/`B004` (stepwise tracking / ordered succession), `س ب ل` — `B001` (extended ways), and `ف ر ق` — `B002`/`B006` (scattering / fork of a road).** **Cue:** singular path follows the joined protections of `6:151–152`, while plural ways scatter travelers from it; `6:154` gives a detailed book as guidance. **Role:** the straight road integrates obligations into one passage, whereas following multiple forks partitions the traveler step by step. **Abductive move:** I infer that ethical coherence is itself the road's singularity; the preceding connected code, singular/plural contrast, and scattering are supplied, while coherence-as-road is mine. **Reading change:** competing paths are dangerous because they disassemble an integrated protection system.

2. **`و ص ي` — `B001`/`B002` (joining / entrusted charge) and `و ق ي` — `B001`/`B002` (protective barrier / self-placement in caution).** **Cue:** the third repetition of the charge joins the path command to hoped-for caution; previous charges aimed at reason and memory. **Role:** charge transmits the integrated route, while caution turns it into a lived barrier against forks. **Abductive move:** I infer a progression from restraint (`6:151`) through maintained memory (`6:152`) to durable route-protection here; repeated endings are supplied, while progression is mine. **Reading change:** *taqwa* is the stabilized operational form of the preceding rational and mnemonic capacities.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:154–155` materialized ethical coherence as an articulated book whose completed joints can be tracked, confirming caution as the lived barrier that preserves one route.

## 6:154 — ثُمَّ ءَاتَيْنَا مُوسَى ٱلْكِتَٰبَ تَمَامًا عَلَى ٱلَّذِىٓ أَحْسَنَ وَتَفْصِيلًۭا لِّكُلِّ شَىْءٍۢ وَهُدًۭى وَرَحْمَةًۭ لَّعَلَّهُم بِلِقَآءِ رَبِّهِمْ يُؤْمِنُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ك ت ب` — `B001`/`B002` (joining / written book), `ت م م` — `B001`/`B007` (completion / completing piece of a weave), `ح س ن` — `B002` (skillful good action), and `ف ص ل` — `B005`/`B013` (joint / clarifying detail).** **Cue:** the book follows the integrated path and three charges of `6:151–153`, and is described as both completion and exhaustive detail; `6:155` then presents this book with the same follow-command. **Role:** completion joins the ethical weave, while detailing preserves articulating joints so totality does not become undifferentiated. **Abductive move:** I infer that revelation completes good practice by making its relations explicit and transmissible; goodness, book, completion, and detail are supplied, while transmission function is mine. **Reading change:** the book is the finished, articulated form of a lived moral architecture.

2. **`ه د ي` — `B001`/`B002` (gentle guidance / intended course), `ر ح م` — `B001`/`B003` (mercy / containing womb), `ل ق ي` — `B004` (face-to-face meeting), and `ء م ن` — `B001`/`B002` (settled trust / assent).** **Cue:** guidance and mercy are directed toward trust in meeting the Lord; `6:158` later marks a point when late belief no longer benefits. **Role:** guidance gives the route, mercy contains development on it, and anticipated meeting supplies the endpoint in which trust must settle before arrival. **Abductive move:** I infer that the book creates a protected preparatory interval for encounter; route, mercy, meeting, and belief are supplied, while preparatory interval is mine. **Reading change:** mercy is temporal-spatial formation for meeting, not exemption from it.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:155–158` clarified the preparatory interval: the same completed book is presently followable, excuses are removed, and delayed assent eventually loses formative benefit.

## 6:155 — وَهَٰذَا كِتَٰبٌ أَنزَلْنَٰهُ مُبَارَكٌۭ فَٱتَّبِعُوهُ وَٱتَّقُوا۟ لَعَلَّكُمْ تُرْحَمُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ك ت ب` — `B001`/`B002` (joining / written book), `ن ز ل` — `B002` (sending down), `ب ر ك` — `B004` (fixed, growing good), and `ت ب ع` — `B003`/`B004` (stepwise tracking / ordered succession).** **Cue:** the demonstrative book continues the completed and detailed book of `6:154`, and its blessing is followed immediately by a command to track it; `6:156–157` removes excuses based on earlier communities or hypothetical guidance. **Role:** sending places the joined articulation within reach, blessing gives it durable increase, and following converts that stable good into an ordered course. **Abductive move:** I infer that blessing grows through enacted succession rather than possession alone; book, blessing, and follow-command are supplied, while the enactment mechanism is mine. **Reading change:** the book's blessedness becomes dynamic: its fixed good propagates as its joints are followed.

2. **`و ق ي` — `B001`/`B002` (protective barrier / self-placement in caution) and `ر ح م` — `B001`/`B003` (mercy / containing womb).** **Cue:** caution is coordinated with following and oriented toward being shown mercy; `6:158` later limits the benefit of belief once decisive signs arrive. **Role:** caution places the follower inside a protective discipline, while mercy supplies the containing environment in which response can mature before closure. **Abductive move:** I infer that the book opens a bounded developmental interval; following, caution, mercy, and the later deadline are supplied, while bounded development is mine. **Reading change:** mercy does not suspend urgency but contains timely formation.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:156–158` confirmed blessed growth as timely enactment, because inherited-distance and hypothetical-guidance excuses collapse before present disclosure and a real response deadline.

## 6:156 — أَن تَقُولُوٓا۟ إِنَّمَآ أُنزِلَ ٱلْكِتَٰبُ عَلَىٰ طَآئِفَتَيْنِ مِن قَبْلِنَا وَإِن كُنَّا عَن دِرَاسَتِهِمْ لَغَٰفِلِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ن ز ل` — `B002` (sending down), `ك ت ب` — `B001`/`B002` (joining / written book), `ط و ف` — `B004` (a bounded group), and `ق ب ل` — `B002` (priorness).** **Cue:** the purpose clause follows the present book of `6:155` and voices a claim that sending was confined to two prior groups. **Role:** the bounded-group image exposes an attempted enclosure of revelation in someone else's earlier sphere, while the newly sent book breaks that enclosure. **Abductive move:** I infer an excuse built by spatializing guidance as both elsewhere and before; prior groups and present sending are supplied, while spatialized excuse is mine. **Reading change:** the complaint is not mere ignorance but an effort to quarantine obligation in another community's past.

2. **`د ر س` — `B001`/`B002` (trace worn by repeated contact / repeated study and retention) and `غ ف ل` — `B001`/`B006` (falling outside attention / remaining unmarked).** **Cue:** their study is paired with claimed heedlessness, and `6:157` immediately replaces both inherited-study and hypothetical-guidance excuses with a present clear disclosure. **Role:** study preserves and repeatedly traverses a textual trace, but heedlessness says that trace never became an attended mark for the speakers. **Abductive move:** I infer that the excuse separates access to a trace from responsibility to attend it; study and heedlessness are supplied, while the access-responsibility split is mine. **Reading change:** heedlessness appears as failed marking, not simply missing information.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:157–158` destroyed the quarantine of obligation: clear disclosure has arrived locally, so claimed heedlessness becomes maintained waiting rather than lack of an accessible mark.

## 6:157 — أَوْ تَقُولُوا۟ لَوْ أَنَّآ أُنزِلَ عَلَيْنَا ٱلْكِتَٰبُ لَكُنَّآ أَهْدَىٰ مِنْهُمْ ۚ فَقَدْ جَآءَكُم بَيِّنَةٌۭ مِّن رَّبِّكُمْ وَهُدًۭى وَرَحْمَةٌۭ ۚ فَمَنْ أَظْلَمُ مِمَّن كَذَّبَ بِـَٔايَٰتِ ٱللَّهِ وَصَدَفَ عَنْهَا ۗ سَنَجْزِى ٱلَّذِينَ يَصْدِفُونَ عَنْ ءَايَٰتِنَا سُوٓءَ ٱلْعَذَابِ بِمَا كَانُوا۟ يَصْدِفُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ن ز ل` — `B002` (sending down), `ك ت ب` — `B001`/`B002` (joining / written book), `ه د ي` — `B001`/`B002` (gentle guidance / intended course), `ج ي ء` — `B001`/`B004` (arrival / bringing and presenting), and `ب ي ن` — `B004`/`B005` (clear appearance / disclosure).** **Cue:** the counterfactual claim that a book would make the speakers better guided is answered by `فقد`: clear disclosure, guidance, and mercy have already arrived from their Lord. **Role:** arrival collapses the hypothetical distance, while disclosure gives the distinction needed to enter an intended course. **Abductive move:** I infer that the verse tests the counterfactual boast by realizing its stated condition; the boast and actual arrival are supplied, while test-by-realization is mine. **Reading change:** the imagined superiority becomes immediately auditable conduct rather than a claim about unrealized circumstances.
