2. **`ظ ل م` — `B004`/`B008` (misplacing / withholding), `ك ذ ب` — `B002` (declaring false), `ء ي ي` — `B002`/`B003` (directed mark / visible sign), `ص د ف` — `B001` (turning away), `ج ز ي` — `B001` (matching recompense), `س و ء` — `B001`/`B002` (badness / harm), and `ع ذ ب` — `B005` (punishment).** **Cue:** declaring the signs false is paired with repeated active turning from them, and the announced recompense is explicitly because that turning persisted. **Role:** denial misplaces a directed mark, while turning withholds the self from its direction; matching recompense returns the structure of that refusal. **Abductive move:** I infer an escalation from verbal cancellation to maintained bodily orientation away, with consequence fitted to the repeated orientation; denial, turning, repetition, and recompense are supplied, while escalation is mine. **Reading change:** rejection is a practiced trajectory, not an isolated proposition.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:158` turned practiced rejection into an explicit delay strategy, confirming that repeated turning away seeks a coercive arrival after refusing the already directed sign.

## 6:158 — هَلْ يَنظُرُونَ إِلَّآ أَن تَأْتِيَهُمُ ٱلْمَلَٰٓئِكَةُ أَوْ يَأْتِىَ رَبُّكَ أَوْ يَأْتِىَ بَعْضُ ءَايَٰتِ رَبِّكَ ۗ يَوْمَ يَأْتِى بَعْضُ ءَايَٰتِ رَبِّكَ لَا يَنفَعُ نَفْسًا إِيمَٰنُهَا لَمْ تَكُنْ ءَامَنَتْ مِن قَبْلُ أَوْ كَسَبَتْ فِىٓ إِيمَٰنِهَا خَيْرًۭا ۗ قُلِ ٱنتَظِرُوٓا۟ إِنَّا مُنتَظِرُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ن ظ ر` — `B002` (waiting or respite), `ء ت ي` — `B001`/`B011` (arrival / being overtaken by ruin), and `ء ي ي` — `B002`/`B003` (directed mark / visible sign).** **Cue:** the opening question construes the rejecters as waiting only for one of three arrivals, and the closing imperative mirrors their waiting; `6:157` has already said disclosure arrived. **Role:** waiting defers response beyond sufficient disclosure until an overtaking arrival removes deliberative distance. **Abductive move:** I infer that delay demands coercive visibility after refusing directed visibility; present clear signs, deferred arrival, and the mirrored wait are supplied, while the visibility contrast is mine. **Reading change:** waiting is exposed as a choice of evidentiary regime, not neutral uncertainty.

2. **`ن ف ع` — `B001` (benefit opposed to harm), `ن ف س` — `B011`/`B012` (living soul-self / the very self), `ء م ن` — `B001`/`B002` (settled trust / assent), `ق ب ل` — `B002` (priorness), `ك س ب` — `B001` (acquiring), and `خ ي ر` — `B001`/`B003` (good / choosing the better).** **Cue:** belief at that arrival does not benefit a self unless trust had settled earlier or the self had acquired good within its belief. **Role:** prior trust and acquired good convert assent into a formed capacity; belated assent lacks the temporal work that could benefit the self. **Abductive move:** I infer that belief has a maturation history evidenced by acquisition, not merely an instantaneous declaration; priorness, settled trust, gain, and benefit are supplied, while maturation history is mine. **Reading change:** the deadline concerns the loss of formative time, not an arbitrary refusal of last-second words.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:159–160` showed what formative belief must resist and produce: it must avoid factional disassembly and acquire acts that survive a precise, personal accounting.

## 6:159 — إِنَّ ٱلَّذِينَ فَرَّقُوا۟ دِينَهُمْ وَكَانُوا۟ شِيَعًۭا لَّسْتَ مِنْهُمْ فِى شَىْءٍ ۚ إِنَّمَآ أَمْرُهُمْ إِلَى ٱللَّهِ ثُمَّ يُنَبِّئُهُم بِمَا كَانُوا۟ يَفْعَلُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ف ر ق` — `B001`/`B002` (distinguishing into parts / dispersing), `د ي ن` — `B001` (obedience and submitted way), and `ش ي ع` — `B002`/`B003` (partisan group / scattering).** **Cue:** partitioning the submitted way produces factions, directly reversing the singular path versus scattering ways of `6:153`; the addressee is disjoined from them in every respect. **Role:** differentiation ceases to clarify and instead cuts an integrated obedience into identity-bearing parties. **Abductive move:** I infer that faction is the social solidification of the forked-path process; partition, parties, and the earlier path contrast are supplied, while social solidification is mine. **Reading change:** sectarian identity is read as an outcome of disassembled practice rather than principled plurality.

2. **`ء م ر` — `B001` (affair), `ن ب ء` — `B002` (consequential reported news), `ك و ن` — `B001` (settled being), and `ف ع ل` — `B001` (act).** **Cue:** their affair is reserved to God, then they will be informed about what they habitually were doing; `6:160` immediately states the matching scale for good and bad acts. **Role:** deferred report gathers factional claims back into an act-based accounting beyond the addressee's jurisdiction. **Abductive move:** I infer that the report strips party labels away and reconstructs the affair from enacted history; reserved affair, later informing, and habitual action are supplied, while label-stripping is mine. **Reading change:** judgment is redirected from group affiliation to the accumulated reality of acts.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:160–161` confirmed label-stripping: matching recompense evaluates presented acts, while the alternative to factional identity is one upright submitted course.

## 6:160 — مَن جَآءَ بِٱلْحَسَنَةِ فَلَهُۥ عَشْرُ أَمْثَالِهَا ۖ وَمَن جَآءَ بِٱلسَّيِّئَةِ فَلَا يُجْزَىٰٓ إِلَّا مِثْلَهَا وَهُمْ لَا يُظْلَمُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ج ي ء` — `B004` (bringing or presenting), `ح س ن` — `B001`/`B002` (goodness / beneficent good action), `ع ش ر` — `B001` (ten), and `م ث ل` — `B001` (likeness and counterpart).** **Cue:** a person comes carrying a good act and receives ten counterparts, whereas the bad act in the coordinated clause receives only its likeness. **Role:** presentation makes the act the accountable object, and numerical counterparting marks an asymmetry that multiplies good without enlarging bad. **Abductive move:** I infer a generosity constrained by precise comparability: the good expands from its presented pattern while the bad is capped at equivalence; ten, likeness, and the clause contrast are supplied, while expansion-versus-cap is mine. **Reading change:** recompense is proportionate without being mechanically symmetrical.

2. **`س و ء` — `B001`/`B002` (badness / harm), `ج ز ي` — `B001` (recompense matching an act), `م ث ل` — `B001` (counterpart), and `ظ ل م` — `B004`/`B008` (misplacing / withholding).** **Cue:** `إلا مثلها` restricts recompense for badness to its counterpart, and the denial of wrongdoing closes the sentence; `6:164` later denies transfer of any burden between selves. **Role:** matching prevents punitive excess, while non-transfer prevents a consequence from being misplaced onto another bearer. **Abductive move:** I infer that justice here has both quantitative and personal boundaries; likeness and no-wronging are supplied, with the personal boundary confirmed later, while their conjunction is mine. **Reading change:** non-oppression becomes a precise anti-excess rule rather than only a general moral assurance.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:164` supplied the personal boundary anticipated here: no self's burden can cross to another, so non-oppression limits both quantity and bearer of recompense.

## 6:161 — قُلْ إِنَّنِى هَدَىٰنِى رَبِّىٓ إِلَىٰ صِرَٰطٍۢ مُّسْتَقِيمٍۢ دِينًۭا قِيَمًۭا مِّلَّةَ إِبْرَٰهِيمَ حَنِيفًۭا ۚ وَمَا كَانَ مِنَ ٱلْمُشْرِكِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ه د ي` — `B001`/`B002` (gentle guidance / intended course), `ص ر ط` — `B001`/`B002` (straight road / passage), `ق و م` — `B008` (straightness and uprightness), and `د ي ن` — `B001` (obedience and submitted way).** **Cue:** guidance terminates in a straight passage appositionally identified as an upright submitted way; `6:162–163` unfolds that way across worship, rite, life, death, and commanded surrender. **Role:** guidance sets direction, the road carries movement, and upright obedience stabilizes the course across domains. **Abductive move:** I infer that the appositions compress orientation, traversal, and rule of life into one mechanism; the grammatical identifications are supplied, while the three-stage compression is mine. **Reading change:** religion is not adjacent to the path but the path's maintained mode of travel.

2. **`م ل ل` — `B003`/`B006` (professed religious way / path worn clear by repeated travel), `ح ن ف` — `B002` (inclination toward a side), and `ش ر ك` — `B001`/`B002` (partnership / associating a partner with God).** **Cue:** Abraham's way is described by a directional inclination and negated association; the preceding ayah made each act personally accountable. **Role:** repeated profession wears a traversable track, while inclination turns that track away from divided allegiance. **Abductive move:** I infer that *hanif* names a corrective lean needed to leave a socially worn partnership-track; way, inclination, and non-association are supplied, while corrective mechanics are mine. **Reading change:** straightness can require active reorientation rather than inert centrality.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:162–164` unfolded the path's maintained mode across worship, rite, life, death, exclusive command, and nontransferable acquisition.

## 6:162 — قُلْ إِنَّ صَلَاتِى وَنُسُكِى وَمَحْيَاىَ وَمَمَاتِى لِلَّهِ رَبِّ ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ص ل و` — `B002`/`B003` (supplication and praise / prescribed worship) and `ن س ك` — `B001`/`B002`/`B003` (devotion / sacrificial offering / ritual act and site).** **Cue:** prayer and rite are first in a four-member possessive series all assigned to God; `6:163` removes partnership and identifies this assignment as commanded. **Role:** prescribed worship orders recurring approach, while *nusuk* concentrates devotion into offered and located action; common assignment prevents either from becoming an autonomous sacred compartment. **Abductive move:** I infer that the paired terms join repeated address to costly embodied offering; prayer, rite, and single assignment are supplied, while that functional pairing is mine. **Reading change:** ritual appears as one articulated mode of total allegiance, not a self-contained religious sector.

2. **`ح ي ي` — `B003` (animate living), `م و ت` — `B001` (loss of vitality and life), `ر ب ب` — `B001`/`B002` (nurturing mastery / governing lordship), and `ع ل م` — `B002` (distinguishing mark).** **Cue:** living and dying extend the same assignment beyond discrete rites, under the Lord of all worlds; `6:164` then makes every self bear only what it acquires. **Role:** the life/death poles encompass the entire temporal span, while lordship holds its many marked worlds under one relation. **Abductive move:** I infer a merism that absorbs every intervening act into the preceding straight course; the two endpoints and total lordship are supplied, while whole-span absorption is mine. **Reading change:** total devotion is temporally exhaustive, not merely an intensification of ritual moments.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:163–165` confirmed whole-span absorption: exclusive assignment is commanded, every intervening act remains personally owned, and every granted station becomes a test.

## 6:163 — لَا شَرِيكَ لَهُۥ ۖ وَبِذَٰلِكَ أُمِرْتُ وَأَنَا۠ أَوَّلُ ٱلْمُسْلِمِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **`ش ر ك` — `B001`/`B002` (sharing partnership / associating a partner with God) and `ء م ر` — `B002` (command or directive).** **Cue:** the verbless negation seals the exclusive assignment of `6:162`, and `بذلك` makes precisely that configuration the commanded object. **Role:** non-partnership prevents life, death, worship, or offering from being apportioned among rival claimants, while command turns exclusivity into obligation. **Abductive move:** I infer that the command governs the architecture of allocation, not only a verbal confession; the fourfold assignment, negated partner, and demonstrative command are supplied, while allocation architecture is mine. **Reading change:** monotheism becomes the rule that keeps a life from being partitioned.

2. **`ء و ل` — `B001` (beginning and precedence) and `س ل م` — `B001`/`B012` (soundness and safety / handing over and consigning).** **Cue:** firstness is predicated of the speaker among those who hand themselves over; `6:164` immediately individualizes acquisition and burden. **Role:** precedence places the speaker at the front of enacted surrender, while handing over restores undivided soundness after partnership is denied. **Abductive move:** I infer exemplary priority rather than a claim detached from conduct: the commanded first-person declaration and surrender are supplied, while exemplar function is mine. **Reading change:** being first is read as taking the first accountable position under the command.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:164–165` made exemplary firstness accountable rather than honorific, because surrender begins with one's own nontransferable burden and continues as tested stewardship.

## 6:164 — قُلْ أَغَيْرَ ٱللَّهِ أَبْغِى رَبًّۭا وَهُوَ رَبُّ كُلِّ شَىْءٍۢ ۚ وَلَا تَكْسِبُ كُلُّ نَفْسٍ إِلَّا عَلَيْهَا ۚ وَلَا تَزِرُ وَازِرَةٌۭ وِزْرَ أُخْرَىٰ ۚ ثُمَّ إِلَىٰ رَبِّكُم مَّرْجِعُكُمْ فَيُنَبِّئُكُم بِمَا كُنتُمْ فِيهِ تَخْتَلِفُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **`غ ي ر` — `B005` (otherness and exception), `ب غ ي` — `B001` (seeking and pursuing), `ر ب ب` — `B001`/`B002` (nurturing mastery / governing lordship), `ك ل ل` — `B003` (totality), and `ش ي ء` — `B001` (a willed or determinate thing).** **Cue:** the rhetorical question opposes seeking an other lord to God's lordship of every thing; it follows the denial of any partner in `6:163`. **Role:** otherness would carve an exception from total lordship, while seeking would actively install that exception as a pursued authority. **Abductive move:** I infer that rival lordship begins as an exception-making operation inside acknowledged totality; otherness, seeking, and every thing are supplied, while exception-making is mine. **Reading change:** association is diagnosed at the level of selectively pursued governance, not only named worship.

2. **`ك س ب` — `B001` (self-acquisition), `ن ف س` — `B011`/`B012` (living soul-self / the very self), `و ز ر` — `B002`/`B004` (heavy burden / supporting bearer), and `ء خ ر` — `B001` (another after the first).** **Cue:** every self's acquisition returns only upon it, and the repeated root in `وازرة وزر` is blocked from crossing to another bearer; `6:165` makes differentiated ranks a test of what each was given. **Role:** acquisition binds consequence to its originating self, while the bearer/burden construction makes non-transfer bodily explicit. **Abductive move:** I infer a provenance rule for moral weight: it follows the act's own line and cannot be reassigned by affiliation; self, acquisition, burden, and other are supplied, while provenance rule is mine. **Reading change:** individuality here means non-transferable causal ownership, not social isolation.

3. **`ر ج ع` — `B001` (return), `ن ب ء` — `B002` (consequential report), `ك و ن` — `B001` (settled being), and `خ ل ف` — `B004` (taking divergent courses).** **Cue:** common return to the Lord leads to being informed about the matters in which habitual divergence persisted. **Role:** return gathers divergent routes to one endpoint, and the report discloses what those routes had become in action. **Abductive move:** I infer that final informing resolves disagreement by exposing its enacted trajectories rather than merely announcing a winning label; return, report, and divergence are supplied, while trajectory disclosure is mine. **Reading change:** disagreement is retained as accountable course-formation.

### Retrospective surprises

Later `6:165` confirmed the provenance rule under unequal conditions: different ranks and gifts alter the test setting but never transfer ownership of the response between selves.

## 6:165 — وَهُوَ ٱلَّذِى جَعَلَكُمْ خَلَٰٓئِفَ ٱلْأَرْضِ وَرَفَعَ بَعْضَكُمْ فَوْقَ بَعْضٍۢ دَرَجَٰتٍۢ لِّيَبْلُوَكُمْ فِى مَآ ءَاتَىٰكُمْ ۗ إِنَّ رَبَّكَ سَرِيعُ ٱلْعِقَابِ وَإِنَّهُۥ لَغَفُورٌۭ رَّحِيمٌۢ

### Activated readings

1. **`ج ع ل` — `B002` (turning into a state), `خ ل ف` — `B001` (successor standing in another's place), and `ء ر ض` — `B001` (the lower ground).** **Cue:** the addressees are made successors of the earth after `6:164` fixed each self's non-transferable responsibility; the surah earlier described destroyed generations being replaced (`6:6`). **Role:** making assigns a state, and succession gives that state inherited place without inherited exemption. **Abductive move:** I infer that earthly tenure is delegated replacement under the memory of prior replacement; successor language and the earlier generation sequence are supplied, while delegated tenure is mine. **Reading change:** being successor marks precarious assignment, not unrestricted possession.

2. **`ر ف ع` — `B001`/`B002` (raising upward / high rank), `ب ع ض` — `B001` (portion of a whole), `ف و ق` — `B001`/`B002` (above in position / higher rank), and `ب ل و` — `B002` (testing that discloses state).** **Cue:** some portions are raised over others by degrees explicitly in order to test them in what they were given. **Role:** rank differentiates positions within one human whole, while testing converts advantage from proof of worth into an instrument that reveals response. **Abductive move:** I infer that inequality is evidentiary rather than self-validating: the gift supplies test conditions, not a verdict; elevation, portions, purpose clause, and testing are supplied, while evidentiary framing is mine. **Reading change:** higher degree increases diagnostic exposure rather than securing moral superiority.

3. **`ء ت ي` — `B002` (granting), `س ر ع` — `B001` (quickness), `ع ق ب` — `B007` (punitive consequence), `غ ف ر` — `B002` (forgiving and shielding from consequence), and `ر ح م` — `B001`/`B003` (mercy / containing womb).** **Cue:** granted differences lead to testing, then the closing balance joins swift consequence with emphatic forgiveness and mercy. **Role:** quick consequence prevents stewardship from becoming indefinite immunity, while forgiveness shields and mercy contains the tested subject without erasing accountability. **Abductive move:** I infer a terminal tension that keeps both urgency and reparative possibility active; speed, punishment, forgiveness, and mercy are supplied, while the regulatory tension is mine. **Reading change:** the surah closes with accountability and restoration operating together, neither reduced to the other.

### Retrospective surprises

No later ayah exists within the surah-boundary-clipped focus window, so no retrospective activation beyond the already integrated earlier context emerged.
